# HANDOFF — Pixel Robot

Entry point for an offline robot project: a Pixel phone provides perception and
reasoning, and an ESP32 controls four mecanum wheels. The native Android app and
the Termux prototype have separate runtime constraints.

This file is a stateless index. Current state, pending work, role allocations,
commands, and historical recovery evidence belong in the documents below.

## Canonical reading order

Canonical documentation and role instructions live in this repository's `docs/`.
Root [AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md), [CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md), and
[GEMINI.md](../GEMINI.md) provide session discovery pointers.

1. Read [WORKFLOW.md](WORKFLOW.md) for the shared process, actual role allocation,
   GitHub access, independent review, and human commit/push gates.
2. Read your role document:

   | Role | Instructions |
   |---|---|
   | Local AI — planning and technical orchestration | [LOCAL_AI.md](LOCAL_AI.md) |
   | Coder / data retriever — phone execution and evidence | [CODER.md](CODER.md) |
   | Reviewer — fresh independent candidate review | [REVIEWER.md](REVIEWER.md) |
   | Doc Keeper — canonical status and append-only decisions | [DOC_KEEPER.md](DOC_KEEPER.md) |
   | Data Engineer — training datasets via pull requests | [DATA_ENGINEER.md](DATA_ENGINEER.md) |

3. Read [STATUS.md](STATUS.md) for prototype state and pending work, or
   [APP_STATUS.md](APP_STATUS.md) for app state and pending work. Use the short
   [decision index](DECISION_INDEX.md) to locate relevant numbered entries;
   [DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md) holds their full text and provenance. Dated
   prototype measurements and the last hardware test are in
   [PROTOTYPE_EVIDENCE.md](PROTOTYPE_EVIDENCE.md).
4. Before implementation, read the applicable [prototype instructions](CLAUDE.md)
   or [Android-app instructions](APP_CLAUDE.md), and [LICENSES.md](LICENSES.md)
   when dependencies are involved. Keep the two runtime scopes separate.
5. Use [COMMANDS.md](COMMANDS.md) for phone operating, calibration, and Git
   inspection, and [OPERATIONS_HISTORY.md](OPERATIONS_HISTORY.md) for dated
   recovery and agent-environment evidence. Use
   [FILES.md](FILES.md) for the dependency map and
   [BENCHMARK_PLAN.md](BENCHMARK_PLAN.md) for benchmarking the existing navigation
   path. For the separate, research-only high-level selector, read
   [benchmark/strategic_selector/README.md](../benchmark/strategic_selector/README.md)
   and its [run index](../benchmark/strategic_selector/RUN_INDEX.md).

Read current source and state rather than relying on a prior session. Report
contradictions without silently choosing a side. If the online thread cannot read
the phone repository, request exact source/output through the executor.

## Gates and documentation discipline

Follow the independent-review and human commit gate in [WORKFLOW.md](WORKFLOW.md).
A reviewer pass never authorizes a commit; push requires separate authorization.
Keep hardware work subject to the human-controlled restrictions in
[COMMANDS.md](COMMANDS.md) and the applicable runtime instructions.

Only Doc Keeper writes canonical status and the decision log. Other roles send
targeted evidence-backed DOC DIFFs as defined in [DOC_KEEPER.md](DOC_KEEPER.md).
Existing decisions remain verbatim. Read the last entry in
[DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md) directly before choosing the next free number.

## Starting a role session

Name the role (Local AI, Coder, Reviewer, or Doc Keeper) in the opening message.
Read the shared workflow, that role's instructions, the applicable current STATUS,
and the decision index in the order above. Open full decisions relevant to the
task. The role file and WORKFLOW define the task-specific gates; do not rely on a
starter prompt copied from an older session.
# WORKFLOW — Pixel Robot roles and commit gate

This is the canonical collaboration workflow. It is procedural, not a Git hook or
an enforced sandbox unless a later decision says a specific mechanism was actually
implemented and tested. Start with `HANDOFF.md`, then this file, then the role file;
read the applicable STATUS file for current facts and `DECISION_INDEX.md` to find
relevant history. Read the full entries in `DECISIONS.md` before relying on them,
and read its last entry directly before numbering a new one.
`AGENTS.md` is the common Codex/Claude/AGY entry point; `CLAUDE.md` and `GEMINI.md`
are minimal pointers. Prototype and app instructions remain separate.

## Roles and allocation

Roles are independent of providers and models. Record the actual assignment at
handoff; do not silently substitute the selected reviewer/model. If one provider
fills multiple roles, use separate role sessions. The coder never reviews its own
work in the same conversation.

| Role | Assignment |
|---|---|
| Local AI: online-app planning and technical orchestration with the human | Online project thread |
| Coder/data retriever: actual phone repo, exact evidence, approved changes and checks | Per task, in the EXECUTION PROFILE |
| Reviewer: fresh independent candidate review | Per task, in the EXECUTION PROFILE |
| Data Engineer: training datasets and their generators, via pull requests ([DATA_ENGINEER.md](DATA_ENGINEER.md)) | Per task, in the EXECUTION PROFILE |
| Doc Keeper: canonical status and append-only decisions | Online project thread; the on-phone documentation executor is assigned per task |

No role has a standing model. The human assigns platform and model when a task
starts, and Local AI writes them into that task's EXECUTION PROFILE. Platforms
the human reports as available: Claude Code CLI, Codex CLI and AGY CLI on the
phone, Jules (Google AI Pro: 100 tasks per rolling 24 h, 15 concurrent) and
Google Colab for training. No exact allowance or reset date is assumed.

Local AI sets objectives, prepares bounded tasks and exact phone commands, requests
source/output from the executor, evaluates evidence and reviews, and helps the
human decide whether to commit. It does not assume phone access or unpushed state.
Coder/data retriever implements and verifies in the actual repository. Reviewer
examines actual candidate changes and surrounding code, not merely the coder's
summary. Doc Keeper owns `STATUS.md`/`APP_STATUS.md` and `DECISIONS.md`; all other
roles send targeted evidence-backed DOC DIFFs.

On the human's instruction, Local AI may author complete role and workflow
files (such as this file, `HANDOFF.md`, or a role file). Doc Keeper verifies
each file's recorded base SHA-256 against `main` before replacing it, then
commits. `STATUS.md`, `APP_STATUS.md` and `DECISIONS.md` remain single-writer:
Local AI still sends DOC DIFFs for them.

## Execution profile

Before Local AI asks the human to open or invoke any phone agent, it must display
the complete profile below and include it in the exact agent prompt and transition
evidence, not only in conversational commentary:

```text
EXECUTION PROFILE
ROLE:
PLATFORM:
MODEL:
EFFORT:
PONYTAIL:
WHY:
FALLBACK:
```

`ROLE` names the exact assignment: Coder, Reviewer, Doc Keeper / documentation
executor, data retriever, or another exact role. `PLATFORM` names the exact surface,
such as Codex CLI, Claude Code CLI, or AGY/Antigravity CLI. `MODEL` gives the exact
selectable identifier or label, never only "best model". `EFFORT` gives the exact
separately selected reasoning effort when the platform exposes one; otherwise use
`N/A` or `platform-controlled`. A word such as `High` inside an AGY model identifier
or label is part of that model name unless AGY separately exposes an effort control.
`PONYTAIL` is exactly `lite`, `full`, `ultra`, or `off`.

If actual models or controls are unknown, Local AI obtains current model-list
evidence before assigning the task. `WHY` is one short line. `FALLBACK` states the
exact replacement platform, model, separately exposed effort, and Ponytail mode.
Platform, model, effort, and Ponytail selection never change the role, its
permissions, or the project authorization gates.

### Ponytail

Verified phone-tool evidence: Ponytail 4.9.0 is installed for Codex CLI, Claude
Code CLI, and AGY/Antigravity CLI; the shared configuration currently specifies
`defaultMode: lite`; and Claude's Ponytail plugin is enabled. Codex Ponytail hook
trust has not been evidenced as completed and must not be described as trusted.
AGY imported Ponytail's six skills and six commands but no lifecycle hooks, so AGY
must not be assumed to activate it automatically; prompts state the intended mode
explicitly whenever Ponytail is required. Installation did not modify this
repository.

- `lite` — ordinary bounded implementation, especially existing or safety-relevant
  Pixel Robot code.
- `full` — deliberate simplification, dependency selection, refactoring, or removal
  of unnecessary abstractions.
- `ultra` — only for explicitly human-approved work that is either a disposable
  experiment or aggressive cleanup. Never use it for safety logic, hardware
  control, authentication, destructive operations, data migration, canonical
  documentation, or independent review.
- `off` — independent correctness/safety review, Doc Keeper work, non-coding
  planning/research, and hardware execution/calibration.

If Codex or Claude automatically starts Ponytail in a Reviewer session, the Reviewer
profile and prompt explicitly switch it `off` before review. An optional Ponytail
over-engineering review may follow the mandatory independent correctness review but
cannot replace it. Ponytail must never simplify away validation, error handling,
security, hardware calibration, required tests or checks, or anything explicitly
required by the task.

`AGENTS.md`, project safety rules, verification requirements, the mandatory
independent review, and the human commit and push gates always override Ponytail.

## Code-change and commit gate

For every code change: (1) Local AI and human define the task; (2) Coder implements
and verifies; (3) Coder automatically invokes the designated independent Reviewer,
without human relay; (4) request includes task, base commit, full candidate diff
including new files, surrounding context and actual checks; (5) freeze edits while
reviewing; (6) Reviewer does not edit, stage, commit, push, run motors, or launch
another reviewer; (7) retain diagnostics separately and return the final review
verbatim, untruncated and separate from Coder commentary; (8) stop for the human's
explicit commit decision after Local AI discussion; (9) any code change after review
requires new review; (10) commit only approved files and push only with separate
authorization; (11) send DOC DIFFs to Doc Keeper.

“Automatically” means no manual relay between applications. It never hides review
findings, failure, costs/limits, or permission errors. Never use blanket permission
bypasses. Reviewer approval is not authorization to commit.

## AGY headless reviewer

Facts observed on this phone during task HEAT-A (2026-10-13, AGY 1.3.3, Coder
evidence in `/termux-home/review/heat_a/`). Earlier text in this section claimed
otherwise; these observations replace it.

**Models change on Google's side.** `gemini-3.1-pro-high` was withdrawn
server-side between 2026-10-12 21:22 and 2026-10-13 04:27 UTC with the same
binary and account; `--model gemini-3.1-pro-high` now fails as "not recognized".
A settings.json model label naming a withdrawn model is silently remapped (the
log showed "Gemini 3.1 Pro (High)" resolved to "Gemini 3.8 Flash (High)"), so a
session can run on a different model without any error. `agy models` on
2026-10-13 listed: `gemini-3.8-flash-high|medium|low`,
`claude-opus-5-5-low|medium|high`, `claude-sonnet-5-5-low|medium|high`,
`gpt-oss-120b-medium`. The `claude-*` entries are the same model family as a
Claude Code Coder. Before every AGY review: run `agy models`, pass `--model`
explicitly with the exact id from the EXECUTION PROFILE, run the smoke call
below with that id, and confirm the model label in the AGY log afterwards.
A missing model is an INCOMPLETE round; a replacement reviewer needs an owner
decision and a new profile.

Smoke call (substitute the profile's model id):
```bash
agy -p 'Reply exactly: REVIEW_SMOKE_PASS. Do not inspect files, run commands, call tools, or make changes.' \
  --model "$AGY_MODEL" --output-format json --sandbox --print-timeout 2m
```
It must return `{"status":"SUCCESS","response":"REVIEW_SMOKE_PASS\n"}`.
Review invocation, run from inside the isolated copy:
```bash
agy -p "$REVIEW_REQUEST" --model "$AGY_MODEL" \
  --output-format json --sandbox --print-timeout 10m
```
`$REVIEW_REQUEST` (keep it under 100 KB; put the diff, context and check output
as files in the copy and name them) contains the frozen task, base, no-mutation
rules and a final-review request. Preserve stdout as the complete JSON review
and stderr as separate diagnostics. It is a direct Coder action, not an
executable helper.

**The shared settings are not neutral.** `/root/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json`
(observed 2026-10-13) has `artifactReviewPolicy: always-proceed`,
`allowNonWorkspaceAccess: true`, 36 command allow entries (including
`git commit` and `git push origin main`), empty deny and ask lists, and trusted
workspaces `/termux-home/robot`, `/termux-home/laya-test` and `/root`. It was
rewritten at 2026-10-13 04:58:50 UTC by a process that is not identified.
Allow-listed commands run without asking, also in headless mode.

**Commands in headless mode.** Nobody can approve a prompt in headless mode, so
commands that are not allow-listed do not run. Allow-listed commands do run.
**`--sandbox` does not confine them in proot:** in HEAT-A round 3 the reviewer
reported that the terminal sandbox failed (`fork/exec /root/.local/bin/agy:
operation not permitted`) and that the allow-listed commands were re-run with the
sandbox bypassed; nobody approved anything and `--dangerously-skip-permissions`
was not used. The commands did run: their results matched the Coder's, and in
round 2 they left `.pyc` files in the copy. Headless workspace reads
and writes are also auto-allowed by default (official
[headless](https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/headless/) documentation). A
prompt is therefore never a read-only boundary. Never use
`--dangerously-skip-permissions`.

**Procedure that produced a valid review (HEAT-A round 3, owner-approved):**
1. No other AGY session runs while the overlay below is in place.
2. Back up settings.json and record its SHA-256.
3. Temporary overlay: allow entries for exactly the check commands the reviewer
   must rerun (absolute interpreter path), and `"deny": ["command(git*)"]`.
   No wildcard interpreter allow. Deny precedes ask precedes allow
   ([permissions](https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/permissions/)).
4. Isolated copy: tracked files plus new candidate files, without `.git` and
   ignored files; check free space first.
5. Before and after the call: SHA-256 of every file in the copy and in the live
   tree (except `.git/`), `git rev-parse HEAD origin/main`,
   `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main`, `git stash list`,
   `git status --porcelain=v1 -uall --ignored`, and the settings.json hash.
   Any difference makes the round INVALID.
6. Checks the reviewer runs must write nothing: `python -B -m py_compile` still
   writes `.pyc` files; use `compile()` instead.
7. The reviewer states each file's line count and quotes the exact code line for
   every file:line it cites. The Coder verifies every citation against the copy;
   a citation to a missing or different line makes the round INVALID (HEAT-A
   round 2 cited lines past the end of a file).
8. Restore settings.json from the backup right after the call and verify the hash.

A true read-only reviewer still needs a dedicated AGY configuration or OS
account, not the shared configuration. Its `settings.json` would use strict,
sandboxed permissions and deny mutation tools:
```json
{"enableTerminalSandbox":true,"toolPermission":"strict","permissions":{"deny":["write_file(*)","command(*)","mcp(*)","execute_url(*)"]}}
```
This dedicated policy is not provisioned or tested; do not add it to the shared
settings, because it would block other AGY role sessions. Authentication, quota,
model, or permission failures mean incomplete review, never a pass. Sources:
[permissions](https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/permissions/) and
[sandbox](https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/sandbox/).

## Data Engineer and fine-tuning track

The Data Engineer works in its own clone of this repository (for example a
cloud coding agent) and only through pull requests; it never pushes to `main`.
Its rules are in `DATA_ENGINEER.md`. Each Data Engineer PR gets two independent
reviews in fresh sessions, Ponytail `off`, by the reviewers assigned for that
task; where possible they come from model families other than the Data
Engineer's. The human merges after Local AI evaluates both reviews with the
human.

Test-set custody: any held-out or independent test set used to pick a model is
kept outside this repository until scoring is finished, because a Data Engineer
agent can read the whole repository. Only its SHA-256, row count and custody location are
recorded in docs beforehand.

Fine-tuning runs in Google Colab. Each run records: notebook or script source
and its SHA-256, Colab GPU type, base model id and revision, dataset SHA-256,
seeds, hyperparameters, training and validation curves, and the SHA-256 of the
output weights. Weights reach the phone by explicit download and are
re-hashed there before any benchmark.

## Timed benchmarks on the phone

Timings are only valid with no agent resident. The Coder prepares a run script
and exits; the human starts it from **native** Termux with `oneshot.sh`
(archived in `benchmark/strategic_selector/ladder/`), which checks that no
Claude/AGY/Codex process runs, logs CPU thermals through Magisk `su`, idles
5 minutes, runs the script inside Debian, and answers each cold-load
page-cache-drop request (root is only reachable from native Termux, not proot).
Runners gate each block on temperature, refuse or redo blocks when Android
moves Termux off cores 4–7 (keep the screen on, Termux in front), and support
`--resume`. Accuracy results are deterministic and may be taken with an agent
resident; timings may not. Every run is archived per `benchmark/INDEX.md`.

coding instructions. You are a fresh independent session, not the Coder continuing
its conversation.

Review the frozen task, base, complete candidate diff including new files, relevant
surrounding source, and actual verification output. Do not accept a summary instead.
Report bugs, regressions, safety problems, missing verification, and uncertainties
with file and line references.

Do not edit, stage, commit, push, run motors, launch another reviewer, or approve a
commit. Return the complete final review. A pass is only a review finding, never
commit permission. Follow the AGY safety limitation in `WORKFLOW.md`.

    43	    "You are the robot's eyes. Using only the information below, describe in one or two short sentences "
    44	    "what is in front of the robot. Do not mention anything that is not listed."
    45	)
    46	
    47	TEMPLATE_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(
    48	    r'</?(?:start|end)(?:_of)+_turn>(?:user|model)?|<(?:eos|bos)>|</?(?:start|end)(?:_of)*_?(?:turn)?$'
    49	)
    50	
    51	
    52	def utc_now():
    53	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
    54	
    55	
    56	def write_json(path, value):
    57	    path = Path(path)
    58	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    59	    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    60	
    61	
    62	def http_json(path, body=None, url=URL, timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT_S):
    63	    data = None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode('utf-8')
    64	    req = urllib.request.Request(url + path, data, {'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    65	    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
    66	        return json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))
    67	
    68	
    69	def discover_photos(root=ROBOT):
    70	    root = Path(root)
    71	    paths = [p for folder in ('bench_photos', 'test_photos') for p in (root / folder).glob('*.jpg')]
    72	    return sorted(paths, key=lambda p: p.relative_to(root).as_posix())
    73	
    74	
    75	def prepare_frame(src_path, dst_path):
    76	    """Open with PIL, exif_transpose, RGB, thumbnail((640, 640), BILINEAR), save JPEG q=85, then re-open."""
    77	    dst_path = Path(dst_path)
    78	    dst_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    79	    with Image.open(src_path) as src:
    80	        img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(src).convert('RGB')
    81	    img.thumbnail((640, 640), Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)
    82	    img.save(dst_path, format='JPEG', quality=85)
    83	    with Image.open(dst_path) as saved:
    84	        return ImageOps.exif_transpose(saved).convert('RGB')
    85	
    86	
    87	def enrich_detections(raw_detections, frame_size):
    88	    """Attach position and coarse distance bucket from detect_person.scene_from_detections to each detection."""
    89	    width, height = frame_size
    90	    enriched = []
    91	    for d in raw_detections:
    92	        _, _, pos, dist, *_ = detect_person.scene_from_detections([d], width, height)[0]
    93	        enriched.append({
    94	            'class_name': str(d['class_name']),
    95	            'conf': float(d['conf']),
    96	            'box_xyxy': [float(x) for x in d['box_xyxy']],
    97	            'position': pos,
    98	            'distance': dist,
    99	        })
   100	    return enriched
   101	
   102	
   103	def select_m2_detections(detections):
   104	    """Reproduce Relate.relations() >32 rule: keep 32 highest-confidence boxes in detector order."""
   105	    if len(detections) > relate.MAX_BOXES:
   106	        keep = sorted(sorted(range(len(detections)), key=lambda i: -detections[i]['conf'])[:relate.MAX_BOXES])
   107	        detections = [detections[i] for i in keep]
   108	    return detections
   109	
   110	
   111	def run_m2_once(head, image, detections):
   112	    """Reproduce Relate.relations() without thread pinning."""
   113	    if len(detections) < 2:
   114	        return {
   115	            'ran': False,
   116	            'boxes_used': len(detections),
   117	            'used_detections': list(detections),
   118	            'rows': [],
   119	            'shown_rows': [],
   120	            'shown_text': relate.TOO_FEW,
   121	            'infer_ms_not_a_measurement': None,
   122	            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   123	        }
   124	    used = select_m2_detections(detections)
   125	    started = time.perf_counter()
   126	    rows = head.infer(image, used)
   127	    ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
   128	    return {
   129	        'ran': True,
   130	        'boxes_used': len(used),
   131	        'used_detections': used,
   132	        'rows': rows,
   133	        'shown_rows': relate.chosen(rows),
   134	        'shown_text': relate.context(rows),
   135	        'infer_ms_not_a_measurement': ms,
   136	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   137	    }
   138	
   139	
   140	def rows_identical(rows_a, rows_b):
   141	    if len(rows_a) != len(rows_b):
   142	        return False
   143	    for a, b in zip(rows_a, rows_b):
   144	        if (a['subject_idx'], a['object_idx'], a['predicate'], a['pass']) != (
   145	            b['subject_idx'], b['object_idx'], b['predicate'], b['pass']
   146	        ):
   147	            return False
   148	        if a['score'] != b['score'] or a['threshold'] != b['threshold']:
   149	            return False
   150	    return True
   151	
   152	
   153	def format_yolo_lines(used_detections):
   154	    return '\n'.join(
   155	        f"- {d['class_name']} [{i}], {d['position']}, {d['distance']}"
   156	        for i, d in enumerate(used_detections)
   157	    )
   158	
   159	
   160	def build_variant_prompts(used_detections, m2_context_text):
   161	    yolo_lines = format_yolo_lines(used_detections)
   162	    body_y_only = f"{INSTRUCTION}\n\n{yolo_lines}"
   163	    body_y_m2 = f"{INSTRUCTION}\n\n{yolo_lines}\n{m2_context_text}"
   164	    return {
   165	        'Y-ONLY': build_prompt('gemma', [{'role': 'user', 'content': body_y_only}]),
   166	        'Y+M2': build_prompt('gemma', [{'role': 'user', 'content': body_y_m2}]),
   167	    }
   168	
   169	
   170	def strip_template_tokens(text):
   171	    return TEMPLATE_TOKEN_RE.sub('', str(text)).strip()
   172	
   173	
   174	def call_gemma(prompt, post=http_json):
   175	    payload = {
   176	        'prompt': prompt,
   177	        'temperature': 0,
   178	        'top_k': 1,
   179	        'n_predict': 96,
   180	        'cache_prompt': False,
   181	        'seed': 0,
   182	        'stop': get_stop_tokens('gemma'),
   183	        'stream': False,
   184	    }
   185	    started = time.perf_counter()
   186	    res = post('/completion', payload)
   187	    ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
   188	    if 'content' not in res:
   189	        raise RuntimeError(f'unexpected /completion response: {res}')
   190	    return strip_template_tokens(res['content']), ms
   191	
   192	
   193	def check_server(get=http_json):
   194	    try:
   195	        health = get('/health')
   196	        props = get('/props')
   197	    except Exception as e:
   198	        raise RuntimeError(
   199	            f'STOP: server on 127.0.0.1:8080 unreachable ({e}). '
   200	            'Ask the owner to start it in native Termux: cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4'
   201	        ) from e
   202	    if health.get('status') != 'ok':
   203	        raise RuntimeError(
   204	            f'STOP: /health returned {health}. '
   205	            'Ask the owner to start it in native Termux: cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4'
   206	        )
   207	    model_path = str(props.get('model_path', ''))
   208	    if Path(model_path).name != EXPECTED_GGUF_NAME:
   209	        raise RuntimeError(
   210	            f'STOP: loaded model is {model_path!r}, expected {EXPECTED_GGUF_NAME}. '
   211	            'Ask the owner to start it in native Termux: cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4'
   212	        )
   213	    gguf_sha256 = None
   214	    for candidate in (model_path, model_path.replace('/data/data/com.termux/files/home', '/termux-home')):
   215	        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
   216	            gguf_sha256 = relate.sha256(candidate)
   217	            break
   218	    dgs = props.get('default_generation_settings', {})
   219	    summary = {
   220	        'model_alias': props.get('model_alias'),
   221	        'model_path': model_path,
   222	        'model_ftype': props.get('model_ftype'),
   223	        'total_slots': props.get('total_slots'),
   224	        'n_ctx': dgs.get('n_ctx'),
   225	        'build_info': props.get('build_info'),
   226	        'bos_token': props.get('bos_token'),
   227	        'eos_token': props.get('eos_token'),
   228	        'modalities': props.get('modalities'),
   229	        'is_sleeping': props.get('is_sleeping'),
   230	    }
   231	    return {'model_path': model_path, 'gguf_sha256': gguf_sha256, 'props_summary': summary}
   232	
   233	
   234	def assign_blind_key(qualifying_photos, seed=BLIND_SEED):
   235	    """Assign Y-ONLY and Y+M2 to X and Y with random.Random(20261017), one coin per photo in sorted photo order."""
   236	    rng = random.Random(seed)
   237	    key = {}
   238	    for photo in sorted(qualifying_photos):
   239	        first = rng.choice(('Y-ONLY', 'Y+M2'))
   240	        second = 'Y+M2' if first == 'Y-ONLY' else 'Y-ONLY'
   241	        key[photo] = {'X': first, 'Y': second}
   242	    return key
   243	
   244	
   245	def annotate_frame(image, used_detections):
   246	    annotated = image.copy()
   247	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(annotated)
   248	    palette = ('#d62728', '#1f77b4', '#2ca02c', '#ff7f0e', '#9467bd', '#17becf', '#e377c2', '#8c564b')
   249	    for i, det in enumerate(used_detections):
   250	        color = palette[i % len(palette)]
   251	        x1, y1, x2, y2 = det['box_xyxy']
   252	        draw.rectangle([x1, y1, x2, y2], outline=color, width=2)
   253	        tx = max(2, min(annotated.width - 80, x1 + 2))
   254	        ty = max(2, min(annotated.height - 18, y1 + 2))
   255	        draw.text((tx, ty), f"{i}: {det['class_name']}", fill=color, stroke_width=2, stroke_fill='white', font_size=14)
   256	    return annotated
   257	
   258	
   259	def _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, x, y, lines, width_chars=96, font_size=14, line_gap=4, fill='#111111'):
   260	    font = ImageFont.load_default(size=font_size)
   261	    for raw_line in lines:
   262	        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(str(raw_line), width=width_chars) if raw_line else ['']
   263	        for part in wrapped:
   264	            draw.text((x, y), part, fill=fill, font=font)
   265	            y += font_size + line_gap
   266	    return y
   267	
   268	
   269	def build_sheet_m2_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, pdf_path):
   270	    """Build sheet_m2.pdf with PIL only: 1 page per >=2-box photo, plus 1 summary page for <2-box photos."""
   271	    qualifying = [r for r in photo_records if r['m2_ran']]
   272	    too_few = [r for r in photo_records if not r['m2_ran']]
   273	    total_pages = len(qualifying) + 1
   274	    pages = []
   275	    page_w, page_h = 900, 1380
   276	
   277	    for idx, rec in enumerate(qualifying, 1):
   278	        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   279	        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
   280	        header = f"{rec['photo']}  --  Page {idx} / {total_pages}"
   281	        sub = (
   282	            f"Frame: {rec['frame_size'][0]}x{rec['frame_size'][1]}  |  "
   283	            f"Detections: {rec['detection_count']} (M2 boxes: {rec['m2_boxes_used']})  |  "
   284	            f"M2 2x identical: {rec['m2_identical_twice']}"
   285	        )
   286	        draw.text((24, 18), header, fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18))
   287	        draw.text((24, 44), sub, fill='#444444', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=13))
   288	
   289	        used_dets = rec.get('m2_detections', rec['detections'][:rec['m2_boxes_used']])
   290	        annotated = annotate_frame(frames_by_photo[rec['photo']], used_dets)
   291	        img_x = (page_w - annotated.width) // 2
   292	        img_y = 68
   293	        page.paste(annotated, (img_x, img_y))
   294	        y = img_y + annotated.height + 16
   295	
   296	        draw.text((24, y), 'Shown M2 text (relate.context):', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15))
   297	        y += 22
   298	        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, rec['m2_shown_text'].splitlines(), width_chars=96, font_size=14)
   299	        y += 10
   300	
   301	        draw.text((24, y), 'Raw top-10 M2 rows (score / threshold / pass):', fill='#000000',
   302	                  font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15))
   303	        y += 22
   304	        if rec['m2_raw_top10']:
   305	            raw_lines = [
   306	                f"{rk:2d}. {r['subject']} [{r['subject_idx']}]  --({r['predicate']})-->  "
   307	                f"{r['object']} [{r['object_idx']}]   score={r['score']:.4f}   thr={r['threshold']:.4f}   pass={r['pass']}"
   308	                for rk, r in enumerate(rec['m2_raw_top10'], 1)
   309	            ]
   310	        else:
   311	            raw_lines = ['(no candidate pairs)']
   312	        _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, raw_lines, width_chars=100, font_size=13)
   313	        pages.append(page)
   314	
   315	    summary_page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   316	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(summary_page)
   317	    draw.text(
   318	        (24, 18),
   319	        f"Summary: Photos with < 2 boxes ({len(too_few)})  --  Page {total_pages} / {total_pages}",
   320	        fill='#000000',
   321	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18),
   322	    )
   323	    y = 56
   324	    if too_few:
   325	        lines = [
   326	            f"- {r['photo']}: {r['detection_count']} box(es)"
   327	            + (f" ({', '.join(r['classes'])})" if r.get('classes') else '')
   328	            for r in too_few
   329	        ]
   330	    else:
   331	        lines = ['None (all processed photos had >= 2 boxes).']
   332	    _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, lines, width_chars=96, font_size=14)
   333	    pages.append(summary_page)
   334	
   335	    pdf_path = Path(pdf_path)
   336	    pdf_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   337	    pages[0].save(pdf_path, format='PDF', resolution=100.0, save_all=True, append_images=pages[1:])
   338	    if pdf_path.stat().st_size >= 20 * 1024 * 1024:
   339	        raise RuntimeError(f'{pdf_path} exceeds 20 MB limit')
   340	
   341	
   342	def build_sheet_ab_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, pdf_path):
   343	    """Build sheet_ab.pdf with PIL only: 1 page per >=2-box photo, numbered boxes + ONLY replies X and Y."""
   344	    qualifying = [r for r in photo_records if r['m2_ran']]
   345	    pages = []
   346	    page_w, page_h = 900, 1200
   347	    total_pages = max(1, len(qualifying))
   348	
   349	    for idx, rec in enumerate(qualifying, 1):
   350	        for forbidden in ('Y-ONLY', 'Y+M2'):
   351	            if forbidden in rec['replies']['X'] or forbidden in rec['replies']['Y']:
   352	                raise RuntimeError(f'variant label leaked into reply for {rec["photo"]}')
   353	        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   354	        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
   355	        header = f"{rec['photo']}  --  Page {idx} / {total_pages}"
   356	        draw.text((24, 18), header, fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18))
   357	
   358	        used_dets = rec.get('m2_detections', rec['detections'][:rec['m2_boxes_used']])
   359	        annotated = annotate_frame(frames_by_photo[rec['photo']], used_dets)
   360	        img_x = (page_w - annotated.width) // 2
   361	        img_y = 54
   362	        page.paste(annotated, (img_x, img_y))
   363	        y = img_y + annotated.height + 24
   364	
   365	        draw.text((24, y), 'Reply X:', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=16))
   366	        y += 24
   367	        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, rec['replies']['X'].splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'],
   368	                                width_chars=90, font_size=15)
   369	        y += 20
   370	
   371	        draw.text((24, y), 'Reply Y:', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=16))
   372	        y += 24
   373	        _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, rec['replies']['Y'].splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'],
   374	                            width_chars=90, font_size=15)
   375	        pages.append(page)
   376	
   377	    if not pages:
   378	        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   379	        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
   380	        draw.text((24, 18), 'No qualifying photos (>= 2 boxes)  --  Page 1 / 1', fill='#000000',
   381	                  font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18))
   382	        pages.append(page)
   383	
   384	    pdf_path = Path(pdf_path)
   385	    pdf_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   386	    pages[0].save(pdf_path, format='PDF', resolution=100.0, save_all=True, append_images=pages[1:])
   387	    if pdf_path.stat().st_size >= 20 * 1024 * 1024:
   388	        raise RuntimeError(f'{pdf_path} exceeds 20 MB limit')
   389	
   390	
   391	def resolve_m2_dir():
   392	    if os.path.isdir(relate.DIRECTORY):
   393	        return relate.DIRECTORY
   394	    if os.path.isdir(FALLBACK_M2_DIR):
   395	        return FALLBACK_M2_DIR
   396	    raise RuntimeError(f'M2 model directory not found at {relate.DIRECTORY} or {FALLBACK_M2_DIR}')
   397	
   398	
   399	def git_base_commit(root=ROBOT):
   400	    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip()
   401	
   402	
   403	def run_harness(limit=None, out_dir=None):
   404	    start_utc = utc_now()
   405	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   406	    if out_dir is None:
   407	        stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
   408	        out_dir = HERE / 'runs' / stamp
   409	    out_dir = Path(out_dir)
   410	    frames_dir = out_dir / 'frames'
   411	    frames_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   412	    log_path = out_dir / 'run.log'
   413	
   414	    def log(msg):
   415	        line = f'[{utc_now()}] {msg}'
   416	        print(line, flush=True)
   417	        with open(log_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as lf:
   418	            lf.write(line + '\n')
   419	
   420	    log(f'START M2-Q1 harness ({TIMING_LABEL})')
   421	    server_info = check_server()
   422	    log(f"Server OK: {server_info['model_path']} (sha256={server_info['gguf_sha256']})")
   423	
   424	    m2_dir = resolve_m2_dir()
   425	    head = relate.Head(directory=m2_dir)
   426	    detector = detect_person.get_detector(pinned=False)
   427	    log(f'Loaded YOLO Detector and M2 Head from {m2_dir}')
   428	
   429	    all_paths = discover_photos(ROBOT)
   430	    all_photo_meta = [
   431	        {
   432	            'photo': p.relative_to(ROBOT).as_posix(),
   433	            'sha256': relate.sha256(p),
   434	            'bytes': p.stat().st_size,
   435	        }
   436	        for p in all_paths
   437	    ]
   438	    selected_paths = all_paths[:limit] if limit is not None else all_paths
   439	    log(f'Discovered {len(all_paths)} total photos; processing {len(selected_paths)} (limit={limit})')
   440	
   441	    staged = []
   442	    frames_by_photo = {}
   443	    failed_photos = []
   444	
   445	    for p in selected_paths:
   446	        rel = p.relative_to(ROBOT).as_posix()
   447	        try:
   448	            src_sha = relate.sha256(p)
   449	            src_bytes = p.stat().st_size
   450	            frame_filename = rel.replace('/', '__')
   451	            frame_path = frames_dir / frame_filename
   452	            decoded = prepare_frame(p, frame_path)
   453	            frames_by_photo[rel] = decoded
   454	
   455	            raw_dets = detector.detect(decoded, 640)
   456	            detections = enrich_detections(raw_dets, decoded.size)
   457	            staged.append({
   458	                'photo': rel,
   459	                'source_sha256': src_sha,
   460	                'source_bytes': src_bytes,
   461	                'frame_file': f'frames/{frame_filename}',
   462	                'frame_sha256': relate.sha256(frame_path),
   463	                'frame_bytes': frame_path.stat().st_size,
   464	                'frame_size': list(decoded.size),
   465	                'detections': detections,
   466	            })
   467	        except Exception as e:
   468	            failed_photos.append({'photo': rel, 'stage': 'frame_or_yolo', 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'})
   469	            log(f'ERROR on {rel}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
   470	
   471	    qualifying_names = [item['photo'] for item in staged if len(item['detections']) >= 2]
   472	    blind_key = assign_blind_key(qualifying_names, seed=BLIND_SEED)
   473	
   474	    photo_records = []
   475	    for item in staged:
   476	        rel = item['photo']
   477	        detections = item['detections']
   478	        decoded = frames_by_photo[rel]
   479	        if len(detections) < 2:
   480	            m2_res = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
   481	            photo_records.append({
   482	                'photo': rel,
   483	                'source_sha256': item['source_sha256'],
   484	                'source_bytes': item['source_bytes'],
   485	                'frame_file': item['frame_file'],
   486	                'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
   487	                'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
   488	                'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
   489	                'detection_count': len(detections),
   490	                'classes': [d['class_name'] for d in detections],
   491	                'm2_ran': False,
   492	                'm2_shown_text': m2_res['shown_text'],
   493	                'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   494	            })
   495	            log(f'{rel}: <2 boxes ({len(detections)}), skipped M2 and Gemma')
   496	            continue
   497	
   498	        m2_1 = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
   499	        m2_2 = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
   500	        m2_same = rows_identical(m2_1['rows'], m2_2['rows'])
   501	        used_dets = m2_1['used_detections']
   502	
   503	        prompts = build_variant_prompts(used_dets, m2_1['shown_text'])
   504	        reply_y_only_1, ms_yo_1 = call_gemma(prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   505	        reply_y_only_2, ms_yo_2 = call_gemma(prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   506	        reply_y_m2_1, ms_ym_1 = call_gemma(prompts['Y+M2'])
   507	        reply_y_m2_2, ms_ym_2 = call_gemma(prompts['Y+M2'])
   508	
   509	        by_variant_1 = {'Y-ONLY': reply_y_only_1, 'Y+M2': reply_y_m2_1}
   510	        by_variant_2 = {'Y-ONLY': reply_y_only_2, 'Y+M2': reply_y_m2_2}
   511	        same_by_variant = {
   512	            'Y-ONLY': reply_y_only_1 == reply_y_only_2,
   513	            'Y+M2': reply_y_m2_1 == reply_y_m2_2,
   514	        }
   515	
   516	        coin = blind_key[rel]
   517	        replies_xy = {'X': by_variant_1[coin['X']], 'Y': by_variant_1[coin['Y']]}
   518	        identical_xy = {'X': same_by_variant[coin['X']], 'Y': same_by_variant[coin['Y']]}
   519	
   520	        rec = {
   521	            'photo': rel,
   522	            'source_sha256': item['source_sha256'],
   523	            'source_bytes': item['source_bytes'],
   524	            'frame_file': item['frame_file'],
   525	            'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
   526	            'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
   527	            'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
   528	            'detection_count': len(detections),
   529	            'detections': detections,
   530	            'm2_detections': [{**d, 'm2_index': i} for i, d in enumerate(used_dets)],
   531	            'm2_ran': True,
   532	            'm2_boxes_used': m2_1['boxes_used'],
   533	            'm2_raw_top10': m2_1['rows'],
   534	            'm2_shown_rows': m2_1['shown_rows'],
   535	            'm2_shown_text': m2_1['shown_text'],
   536	            'm2_identical_twice': m2_same,
   537	            'gemma_prompts': prompts,
   538	            'replies': replies_xy,
   539	            'replies_identical_twice': identical_xy,
   540	            'timings_not_a_measurement': {
   541	                'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   542	                'm2_ms_run1': m2_1['infer_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   543	                'm2_ms_run2': m2_2['infer_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   544	                'gemma_total_ms_not_a_measurement': ms_yo_1 + ms_yo_2 + ms_ym_1 + ms_ym_2,
   545	            },
   546	            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   547	        }
   548	        if not m2_same:
   549	            rec['m2_raw_top10_run2'] = m2_2['rows']
   550	        if not all(identical_xy.values()):
   551	            rec['replies_run2'] = {'X': by_variant_2[coin['X']], 'Y': by_variant_2[coin['Y']]}
   552	
   553	        photo_records.append(rec)
   554	        log(
   555	            f"{rel}: boxes={len(detections)} (used={m2_1['boxes_used']}), "
   556	            f"chosen={len(m2_1['shown_rows'])}, m2_2x={m2_same}, "
   557	            f"gemma_2x={all(identical_xy.values())} ({TIMING_LABEL})"
   558	        )
   559	
   560	    sheet_m2_path = out_dir / 'sheet_m2.pdf'
   561	    sheet_ab_path = out_dir / 'sheet_ab.pdf'
   562	    build_sheet_m2_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, sheet_m2_path)
   563	    build_sheet_ab_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, sheet_ab_path)
   564	    log(f'Built PDFs: {sheet_m2_path.name} ({sheet_m2_path.stat().st_size} B), {sheet_ab_path.name} ({sheet_ab_path.stat().st_size} B)')
   565	
   566	    end_utc = utc_now()
   567	    elapsed_s = time.perf_counter() - t0
   568	
   569	    qualifying_records = [r for r in photo_records if r['m2_ran']]
   570	    counts = {
   571	        'total_discovered': len(all_paths),
   572	        'processed': len(photo_records),
   573	        'ge_2_boxes': len(qualifying_records),
   574	        'lt_2_boxes': sum(1 for r in photo_records if not r['m2_ran']),
   575	        'gt_32_boxes': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['detection_count'] > relate.MAX_BOXES),
   576	        'm2_identical_twice': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['m2_identical_twice']),
   577	        'gemma_x_identical_twice': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['replies_identical_twice']['X']),
   578	        'gemma_y_identical_twice': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['replies_identical_twice']['Y']),
   579	        'gemma_both_identical_twice': sum(
   580	            1 for r in qualifying_records if all(r['replies_identical_twice'].values())
   581	        ),
   582	        'failed': len(failed_photos),
   583	    }
   584	
   585	    results_payload = {
   586	        'task': 'M2-Q1',
   587	        'accuracy_label': ACCURACY_LABEL,
   588	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   589	        'instruction': INSTRUCTION,
   590	        'counts': counts,
   591	        'failed_photos': failed_photos,
   592	        'photos': photo_records,
   593	    }
   594	    write_json(out_dir / 'results.json', results_payload)
   595	    write_json(out_dir / 'key.json', blind_key)
   596	
   597	    imported_files = (
   598	        'benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py',
   599	        'relate.py',
   600	        'detect_person.py',
   601	        'picker.py',
   602	        'chat.py',
   603	        'server_manager.py',
   604	    )
   605	    manifest = {
   606	        'task': 'M2-Q1',
   607	        'accuracy_label': ACCURACY_LABEL,
   608	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   609	        'base_commit': git_base_commit(ROBOT),
   610	        'start_utc': start_utc,
   611	        'end_utc': end_utc,
   612	        'elapsed_s_not_a_measurement': elapsed_s,
   613	        'limit': limit,
   614	        'code_sha256': {rel_p: relate.sha256(ROBOT / rel_p) for rel_p in imported_files},
   615	        'm2_files': {
   616	            'directory': m2_dir,
   617	            'sha256': {name: relate.sha256(os.path.join(m2_dir, name)) for name in sorted(relate.HASHES)},
   618	        },
   619	        'yolo_files': {
   620	            str(size): {'path': path, 'sha256': relate.sha256(path)}
   621	            for size, path in sorted(detect_person.MODELS.items())
   622	        },
   623	        'gguf': {
   624	            'path': server_info['model_path'],
   625	            'sha256': server_info['gguf_sha256'],
   626	        },
   627	        'server_props': server_info['props_summary'],
   628	        'interpreter': {
   629	            'executable': sys.executable,
   630	            'python_version': sys.version.split()[0],
   631	            'platform': sys.platform,
   632	            'onnxruntime_version': ort.__version__,
   633	            'numpy_version': np.__version__,
   634	            'pil_version': Image.__version__,
   635	        },
   636	        'counts': counts,
   637	        'failed_photos': failed_photos,
   638	        'processed_photos': [
   639	            {
   640	                'photo': r['photo'],
   641	                'source_sha256': r['source_sha256'],
   642	                'source_bytes': r['source_bytes'],
   643	                'frame_file': r['frame_file'],
   644	                'frame_sha256': r['frame_sha256'],
   645	                'frame_bytes': r['frame_bytes'],
   646	                'frame_size': r['frame_size'],
   647	            }
   648	            for r in photo_records
   649	        ],
   650	        'all_discovered_photos': all_photo_meta,
   651	    }
   652	    write_json(out_dir / 'manifest.json', manifest)
   653	    log(f'DONE M2-Q1 harness in {elapsed_s:.2f}s ({TIMING_LABEL}) -> {out_dir}')
   654	    return out_dir
   655	
   656	
   657	def main(argv=None):
   658	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   659	    parser.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=None, help='Process only the first N sorted photos (smoke run)')
   660	    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path, default=None, help='Output directory (default: runs/<UTC>)')
   661	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   662	    if args.limit is not None and args.limit < 1:
   663	        parser.error('--limit must be >= 1')
   664	    run_harness(limit=args.limit, out_dir=args.out)
   665	
   666

   668	    main()

     1	# RelateAnything M2 Quality & Gemma A/B Desk Test (`M2-Q1`)
     2	
     3	**Status labels:**
     4	- **Accuracy VALID (temperature 0); timings NOT measurements (agent resident); grading pending (Local AI, blind).**
     5	- Motors stay off. No robot files are modified.
     6	
     7	## Purpose
     8	
     9	Evaluates on every `*.jpg` in `bench_photos/` and `test_photos/` (shrunk to 640 long side, saved at JPEG quality 85, and re-opened):
    10	1. Whether RelateAnything M2 (`relsgg-vits16`, fp32, via `relate.Head` with SHA-256 verification and without thread pinning) produces accurate object-relation lines (`sheet_m2.pdf`).
    11	2. Whether adding M2 relation lines (`Y+M2`) helps Gemma 4 E2B Q4_0 (`setup_q4`) describe a scene better than YOLO detections alone (`Y-ONLY`), presented blind as `X` and `Y` (`sheet_ab.pdf`).
    12	
    13	Grading is performed blind by Local AI from `sheet_m2.pdf` and `sheet_ab.pdf`. The harness does not grade replies.
    14	
    15	## Prerequisites
    16	
    17	1. Ensure the robot default server (`Gemma 4 E2B Q4_0`) is running on `127.0.0.1:8080` in native Termux:
    18	   ```bash
    19	   cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4
    20	   ```
    21	2. Use the Termux Python interpreter (`/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3`), which provides `onnxruntime`, `numpy`, and `PIL`.
    22	
    23	## How to Run
    24	
    25	Run offline unit tests (fakes only, no network or model files required):
    26	```bash
    27	/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/test_quality1.py -v
    28	```
    29	
    30	Smoke run (first 3 sorted photos):
    31	```bash
    32	/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py --limit 3
    33	```
    34	
    35	Full run (all sorted photos in `bench_photos/` and `test_photos/`):
    36	```bash
    37	/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py
    38	```
    39	
    40	## Outputs (`runs/<UTC>/`)
    41	
    42	- `frames/` — 640-long-side JPEG (quality 85) frames used by YOLO and M2.
    43	- `results.json` — per-photo hashes, detections, M2 raw top-10 rows and shown text, 2× determinism flags, Gemma prompts, and blind replies `X` and `Y`.
    44	- `key.json` — blind mapping `photo -> {"X": "Y-ONLY"|"Y+M2", "Y": ...}` generated with `random.Random(20261017)` (never included in PDFs or reports).
    45	- `sheet_m2.pdf` — one page per `>= 2`-box photo showing the numbered frame, shown M2 text, and raw top-10 M2 rows, plus one summary page listing `< 2`-box photos.
    46	- `sheet_ab.pdf` — one page per `>= 2`-box photo showing the numbered frame and only the blind replies `X` and `Y`.
    47	- `manifest.json` — base commit, file SHA-256 hashes, model/GGUF hashes, `/props` summary, interpreter versions, photo hashes, and UTC timestamps.
    48	- `run.log` — run progress log.
