    57	+
    58	+def utc_now():
    59	+    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
    60	+
    61	+
    62	+def write_json(path, value):
    63	+    path = Path(path)
    64	+    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    65	+    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    66	+
    67	+
    68	+def http_json(path, body=None, url=URL, timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT_S):
    69	+    data = None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode('utf-8')
    70	+    req = urllib.request.Request(url + path, data, {'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    71	+    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
    72	+        return json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))
    73	+
    74	+
    75	+def discover_photos(root=ROBOT):
    76	+    root = Path(root)
    77	+    paths = [p for folder in ('bench_photos', 'test_photos') for p in (root / folder).glob('*.jpg')]
    78	+    return sorted(paths, key=lambda p: p.relative_to(root).as_posix())
    79	+
    80	+
    81	+def prepare_frame(src_path, dst_path):
    82	+    """Open with PIL, exif_transpose, RGB, thumbnail((640, 640), BILINEAR), save JPEG q=85, then re-open."""
    83	+    dst_path = Path(dst_path)
    84	+    dst_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    85	+    with Image.open(src_path) as src:
    86	+        img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(src).convert('RGB')
    87	+    img.thumbnail((640, 640), Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)
    88	+    img.save(dst_path, format='JPEG', quality=85)
    89	+    with Image.open(dst_path) as saved:
    90	+        return ImageOps.exif_transpose(saved).convert('RGB')
    91	+
    92	+
    93	+def enrich_detections(raw_detections, frame_size):
    94	+    """Attach position and coarse distance bucket from detect_person.scene_from_detections to each detection."""
    95	+    width, height = frame_size
    96	+    enriched = []
    97	+    for d in raw_detections:
    98	+        _, _, pos, dist, *_ = detect_person.scene_from_detections([d], width, height)[0]
    99	+        enriched.append({
   100	+            'class_name': str(d['class_name']),
   101	+            'conf': float(d['conf']),
   102	+            'box_xyxy': [float(x) for x in d['box_xyxy']],
   103	+            'position': pos,
   104	+            'distance': dist,
   105	+        })
   106	+    return enriched
   107	+
   108	+
   109	+def select_m2_detections(detections):
   110	+    """Reproduce Relate.relations() >32 rule: keep 32 highest-confidence boxes in detector order."""
   111	+    if len(detections) > relate.MAX_BOXES:
   112	+        keep = sorted(sorted(range(len(detections)), key=lambda i: -detections[i]['conf'])[:relate.MAX_BOXES])
   113	+        detections = [detections[i] for i in keep]
   114	+    return detections
   115	+
   116	+
   117	+def run_m2_once(head, image, detections):
   118	+    """Reproduce Relate.relations() without thread pinning."""
   119	+    if len(detections) < 2:
   120	+        return {
   121	+            'ran': False,
   122	+            'boxes_used': len(detections),
   123	+            'used_detections': list(detections),
   124	+            'rows': [],
   125	+            'shown_rows': [],
   126	+            'shown_text': relate.TOO_FEW,
   127	+            'infer_ms_not_a_measurement': None,
   128	+            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   129	+        }
   130	+    used = select_m2_detections(detections)
   131	+    started = time.perf_counter()
   132	+    rows = head.infer(image, used)
   133	+    ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
   134	+    return {
   135	+        'ran': True,
   136	+        'boxes_used': len(used),
   137	+        'used_detections': used,
   138	+        'rows': rows,
   139	+        'shown_rows': relate.chosen(rows),
   140	+        'shown_text': relate.context(rows),
   141	+        'infer_ms_not_a_measurement': ms,
   142	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   143	+    }
   144	+
   145	+
   146	+def rows_identical(rows_a, rows_b):
   147	+    if len(rows_a) != len(rows_b):
   148	+        return False
   149	+    for a, b in zip(rows_a, rows_b):
   150	+        if (a['subject_idx'], a['object_idx'], a['predicate'], a['pass']) != (
   151	+            b['subject_idx'], b['object_idx'], b['predicate'], b['pass']
   152	+        ):
   153	+            return False
   154	+        if a['score'] != b['score'] or a['threshold'] != b['threshold']:
   155	+            return False
   156	+    return True
   157	+
   158	+
   159	+def format_yolo_lines(used_detections):
   160	+    return '\n'.join(
   161	+        f"- {d['class_name']} [{i}], {d['position']}, {d['distance']}"
   162	+        for i, d in enumerate(used_detections)
   163	+    )
   164	+
   165	+
   166	+def build_variant_prompts(used_detections, m2_context_text):
   167	+    yolo_lines = format_yolo_lines(used_detections)
   168	+    body_y_only = f"{INSTRUCTION}\n\n{yolo_lines}"
   169	+    body_y_m2 = f"{INSTRUCTION}\n\n{yolo_lines}\n{m2_context_text}"
   170	+    return {
   171	+        'Y-ONLY': build_prompt('gemma', [{'role': 'user', 'content': body_y_only}]),
   172	+        'Y+M2': build_prompt('gemma', [{'role': 'user', 'content': body_y_m2}]),
   173	+    }
   174	+
   175	+
   176	+def strip_template_tokens(text):
   177	+    return TEMPLATE_TOKEN_RE.sub('', str(text)).strip()
   178	+
   179	+
   180	+def call_gemma(prompt, post=http_json):
   181	+    payload = {
   182	+        'prompt': prompt,
   183	+        'temperature': 0,
   184	+        'top_k': 1,
   185	+        'n_predict': 96,
   186	+        'cache_prompt': False,
   187	+        'seed': 0,
   188	+        'stop': get_stop_tokens('gemma'),
   189	+        'stream': False,
   190	+    }
   191	+    started = time.perf_counter()
   192	+    res = post('/completion', payload)
   193	+    ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
   194	+    if 'content' not in res:
   195	+        raise RuntimeError(f'unexpected /completion response: {res}')
   196	+    return strip_template_tokens(res['content']), ms
   197	+
   198	+
   199	+def check_server(get=http_json):
   200	+    try:
   201	+        health = get('/health')
   202	+        props = get('/props')
   203	+    except Exception as e:
   204	+        raise RuntimeError(
   205	+            f'STOP: server on 127.0.0.1:8080 unreachable ({e}). '
   206	+            'Ask the owner to start it in native Termux: cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4'
   207	+        ) from e
   208	+    if health.get('status') != 'ok':
   209	+        raise RuntimeError(
   210	+            f'STOP: /health returned {health}. '
   211	+            'Ask the owner to start it in native Termux: cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4'
   212	+        )
   213	+    model_path = str(props.get('model_path', ''))
   214	+    if Path(model_path).name != EXPECTED_GGUF_NAME:
   215	+        raise RuntimeError(
   216	+            f'STOP: loaded model is {model_path!r}, expected {EXPECTED_GGUF_NAME}. '
   217	+            'Ask the owner to start it in native Termux: cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4'
   218	+        )
   219	+    gguf_sha256 = None
   220	+    for candidate in (model_path, model_path.replace('/data/data/com.termux/files/home', '/termux-home')):
   221	+        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
   222	+            gguf_sha256 = relate.sha256(candidate)
   223	+            break
   224	+    dgs = props.get('default_generation_settings', {})
   225	+    summary = {
   226	+        'model_alias': props.get('model_alias'),
   227	+        'model_path': model_path,
   228	+        'model_ftype': props.get('model_ftype'),
   229	+        'total_slots': props.get('total_slots'),
   230	+        'n_ctx': dgs.get('n_ctx'),
   231	+        'build_info': props.get('build_info'),
   232	+        'bos_token': props.get('bos_token'),
   233	+        'eos_token': props.get('eos_token'),
   234	+        'modalities': props.get('modalities'),
   235	+        'is_sleeping': props.get('is_sleeping'),
   236	+    }
   237	+    return {'model_path': model_path, 'gguf_sha256': gguf_sha256, 'props_summary': summary}
   238	+
   239	+
   240	+def assign_blind_key(qualifying_photos, seed=BLIND_SEED):
   241	+    """Assign Y-ONLY and Y+M2 to X and Y with random.Random(20261017), one coin per photo in sorted photo order."""
   242	+    rng = random.Random(seed)
   243	+    key = {}
   244	+    for photo in sorted(qualifying_photos):
   245	+        first = rng.choice(('Y-ONLY', 'Y+M2'))
   246	+        second = 'Y+M2' if first == 'Y-ONLY' else 'Y-ONLY'
   247	+        key[photo] = {'X': first, 'Y': second}
   248	+    return key
   249	+
   250	+
   251	+def annotate_frame(image, used_detections):
   252	+    annotated = image.copy()
   253	+    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(annotated)
   254	+    palette = ('#d62728', '#1f77b4', '#2ca02c', '#ff7f0e', '#9467bd', '#17becf', '#e377c2', '#8c564b')
   255	+    for i, det in enumerate(used_detections):
   256	+        color = palette[i % len(palette)]
   257	+        x1, y1, x2, y2 = det['box_xyxy']
   258	+        draw.rectangle([x1, y1, x2, y2], outline=color, width=2)
   259	+        tx = max(2, min(annotated.width - 80, x1 + 2))
   260	+        ty = max(2, min(annotated.height - 18, y1 + 2))
   261	+        draw.text((tx, ty), f"{i}: {det['class_name']}", fill=color, stroke_width=2, stroke_fill='white', font_size=14)
   262	+    return annotated
   263	+
   264	+
   265	+def _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, x, y, lines, width_chars=96, font_size=14, line_gap=4, fill='#111111'):
   266	+    font = ImageFont.load_default(size=font_size)
   267	+    for raw_line in lines:
   268	+        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(str(raw_line), width=width_chars) if raw_line else ['']
   269	+        for part in wrapped:
   270	+            draw.text((x, y), part, fill=fill, font=font)
   271	+            y += font_size + line_gap
   272	+    return y
   273	+
   274	+
   275	+def build_sheet_m2_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, pdf_path):
   276	+    """Build sheet_m2.pdf with PIL only: 1 page per >=2-box photo, plus 1 summary page for <2-box photos."""
   277	+    qualifying = [r for r in photo_records if r['m2_ran']]
   278	+    too_few = [r for r in photo_records if not r['m2_ran']]
   279	+    total_pages = len(qualifying) + 1
   280	+    pages = []
   281	+    page_w, page_h = 900, 1380
   282	+
   283	+    for idx, rec in enumerate(qualifying, 1):
   284	+        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   285	+        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
   286	+        header = f"{rec['photo']}  --  Page {idx} / {total_pages}"
   287	+        sub = (
   288	+            f"Frame: {rec['frame_size'][0]}x{rec['frame_size'][1]}  |  "
   289	+            f"Detections: {rec['detection_count']} (M2 boxes: {rec['m2_boxes_used']})  |  "
   290	+            f"M2 2x identical: {rec['m2_identical_twice']}"
   291	+        )
   292	+        draw.text((24, 18), header, fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18))
   293	+        draw.text((24, 44), sub, fill='#444444', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=13))
   294	+
   295	+        used_dets = rec.get('m2_detections', rec['detections'][:rec['m2_boxes_used']])
   296	+        annotated = annotate_frame(frames_by_photo[rec['photo']], used_dets)
   297	+        img_x = (page_w - annotated.width) // 2
   298	+        img_y = 68
   299	+        page.paste(annotated, (img_x, img_y))
   300	+        y = img_y + annotated.height + 16
   301	+
   302	+        draw.text((24, y), 'Shown M2 text (relate.context):', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15))
   303	+        y += 22
   304	+        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, rec['m2_shown_text'].splitlines(), width_chars=96, font_size=14)
   305	+        y += 10
   306	+
   307	+        draw.text((24, y), 'Raw top-10 M2 rows (score / threshold / pass):', fill='#000000',
   308	+                  font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15))
   309	+        y += 22
   310	+        if rec['m2_raw_top10']:
   311	+            raw_lines = [
   312	+                f"{rk:2d}. {r['subject']} [{r['subject_idx']}]  --({r['predicate']})-->  "
   313	+                f"{r['object']} [{r['object_idx']}]   score={r['score']:.4f}   thr={r['threshold']:.4f}   pass={r['pass']}"
   314	+                for rk, r in enumerate(rec['m2_raw_top10'], 1)
   315	+            ]
   316	+        else:
   317	+            raw_lines = ['(no candidate pairs)']
   318	+        _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, raw_lines, width_chars=100, font_size=13)
   319	+        pages.append(page)
   320	+
   321	+    summary_page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   322	+    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(summary_page)
   323	+    draw.text(
   324	+        (24, 18),
   325	+        f"Summary: Photos with < 2 boxes ({len(too_few)})  --  Page {total_pages} / {total_pages}",
   326	+        fill='#000000',
   327	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18),
   328	+    )
   329	+    y = 56
   330	+    if too_few:
   331	+        lines = [
   332	+            f"- {r['photo']}: {r['detection_count']} box(es)"
   333	+            + (f" ({', '.join(r['classes'])})" if r.get('classes') else '')
   334	+            for r in too_few
   335	+        ]
   336	+    else:
   337	+        lines = ['None (all processed photos had >= 2 boxes).']
   338	+    _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, lines, width_chars=96, font_size=14)
   339	+    pages.append(summary_page)
   340	+
   341	+    pdf_path = Path(pdf_path)
   342	+    pdf_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   343	+    pages[0].save(pdf_path, format='PDF', resolution=100.0, save_all=True, append_images=pages[1:])
   344	+    if pdf_path.stat().st_size >= 20 * 1024 * 1024:
   345	+        raise RuntimeError(f'{pdf_path} exceeds 20 MB limit')
   346	+
   347	+
   348	+def build_sheet_ab_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, pdf_path):
   349	+    """Build sheet_ab.pdf with PIL only: 1 page per >=2-box photo, numbered boxes + ONLY replies X and Y."""
   350	+    qualifying = [r for r in photo_records if r['m2_ran']]
   351	+    pages = []
   352	+    page_w, page_h = 900, 1200
   353	+    total_pages = max(1, len(qualifying))
   354	+
   355	+    for idx, rec in enumerate(qualifying, 1):
   356	+        for forbidden in ('Y-ONLY', 'Y+M2'):
   357	+            if forbidden in rec['replies']['X'] or forbidden in rec['replies']['Y']:
   358	+                raise RuntimeError(f'variant label leaked into reply for {rec["photo"]}')
   359	+        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   360	+        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
   361	+        header = f"{rec['photo']}  --  Page {idx} / {total_pages}"
   362	+        draw.text((24, 18), header, fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18))
   363	+
   364	+        used_dets = rec.get('m2_detections', rec['detections'][:rec['m2_boxes_used']])
   365	+        annotated = annotate_frame(frames_by_photo[rec['photo']], used_dets)
   366	+        img_x = (page_w - annotated.width) // 2
   367	+        img_y = 54
   368	+        page.paste(annotated, (img_x, img_y))
   369	+        y = img_y + annotated.height + 24
   370	+
   371	+        draw.text((24, y), 'Reply X:', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=16))
   372	+        y += 24
   373	+        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, rec['replies']['X'].splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'],
   374	+                                width_chars=90, font_size=15)
   375	+        y += 20
   376	+
   377	+        draw.text((24, y), 'Reply Y:', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=16))
   378	+        y += 24
   379	+        _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, rec['replies']['Y'].splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'],
   380	+                            width_chars=90, font_size=15)
   381	+        pages.append(page)
   382	+
   383	+    if not pages:
   384	+        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
   385	+        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
   386	+        draw.text((24, 18), 'No qualifying photos (>= 2 boxes)  --  Page 1 / 1', fill='#000000',
   387	+                  font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18))
   388	+        pages.append(page)
   389	+
   390	+    pdf_path = Path(pdf_path)
   391	+    pdf_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   392	+    pages[0].save(pdf_path, format='PDF', resolution=100.0, save_all=True, append_images=pages[1:])
   393	+    if pdf_path.stat().st_size >= 20 * 1024 * 1024:
   394	+        raise RuntimeError(f'{pdf_path} exceeds 20 MB limit')
   395	+
   396	+
   397	+def resolve_m2_dir():
   398	+    if os.path.isdir(relate.DIRECTORY):
   399	+        return relate.DIRECTORY
   400	+    if os.path.isdir(FALLBACK_M2_DIR):
   401	+        return FALLBACK_M2_DIR
   402	+    raise RuntimeError(f'M2 model directory not found at {relate.DIRECTORY} or {FALLBACK_M2_DIR}')
   403	+
   404	+
   405	+def git_base_commit(root=ROBOT):
   406	+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip()
   407	+
   408	+
   409	+def run_harness(limit=None, out_dir=None):
   410	+    start_utc = utc_now()
   411	+    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   412	+    if out_dir is None:
   413	+        stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
   414	+        out_dir = HERE / 'runs' / stamp
   415	+    out_dir = Path(out_dir)
   416	+    frames_dir = out_dir / 'frames'
   417	+    frames_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   418	+    log_path = out_dir / 'run.log'
   419	+
   420	+    def log(msg):
   421	+        line = f'[{utc_now()}] {msg}'
   422	+        print(line, flush=True)
   423	+        with open(log_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as lf:
   424	+            lf.write(line + '\n')
   425	+
   426	+    log(f'START M2-Q1 harness ({TIMING_LABEL})')
   427	+    server_info = check_server()
   428	+    log(f"Server OK: {server_info['model_path']} (sha256={server_info['gguf_sha256']})")
   429	+
   430	+    m2_dir = resolve_m2_dir()
   431	+    head = relate.Head(directory=m2_dir)
   432	+    detector = detect_person.get_detector(pinned=False)
   433	+    log(f'Loaded YOLO Detector and M2 Head from {m2_dir}')
   434	+
   435	+    all_paths = discover_photos(ROBOT)
   436	+    all_photo_meta = [
   437	+        {
   438	+            'photo': p.relative_to(ROBOT).as_posix(),
   439	+            'sha256': relate.sha256(p),
   440	+            'bytes': p.stat().st_size,
   441	+        }
   442	+        for p in all_paths
   443	+    ]
   444	+    selected_paths = all_paths[:limit] if limit is not None else all_paths
   445	+    log(f'Discovered {len(all_paths)} total photos; processing {len(selected_paths)} (limit={limit})')
   446	+
   447	+    staged = []
   448	+    frames_by_photo = {}
   449	+    failed_photos = []
   450	+
   451	+    for p in selected_paths:
   452	+        rel = p.relative_to(ROBOT).as_posix()
   453	+        try:
   454	+            src_sha = relate.sha256(p)
   455	+            src_bytes = p.stat().st_size
   456	+            frame_filename = rel.replace('/', '__')
   457	+            frame_path = frames_dir / frame_filename
   458	+            decoded = prepare_frame(p, frame_path)
   459	+            frames_by_photo[rel] = decoded
   460	+
   461	+            raw_dets = detector.detect(decoded, 640)
   462	+            detections = enrich_detections(raw_dets, decoded.size)
   463	+            staged.append({
   464	+                'photo': rel,
   465	+                'source_sha256': src_sha,
   466	+                'source_bytes': src_bytes,
   467	+                'frame_file': f'frames/{frame_filename}',
   468	+                'frame_sha256': relate.sha256(frame_path),
   469	+                'frame_bytes': frame_path.stat().st_size,
   470	+                'frame_size': list(decoded.size),
   471	+                'detections': detections,
   472	+            })
   473	+        except Exception as e:
   474	+            failed_photos.append({'photo': rel, 'stage': 'frame_or_yolo', 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'})
   475	+            log(f'ERROR on {rel}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
   476	+
   477	+    qualifying_names = [item['photo'] for item in staged if len(item['detections']) >= 2]
   478	+    blind_key = assign_blind_key(qualifying_names, seed=BLIND_SEED)
   479	+
   480	+    photo_records = []
   481	+    for item in staged:
   482	+        rel = item['photo']
   483	+        detections = item['detections']
   484	+        decoded = frames_by_photo[rel]
   485	+        if len(detections) < 2:
   486	+            m2_res = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
   487	+            photo_records.append({
   488	+                'photo': rel,
   489	+                'source_sha256': item['source_sha256'],
   490	+                'source_bytes': item['source_bytes'],
   491	+                'frame_file': item['frame_file'],
   492	+                'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
   493	+                'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
   494	+                'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
   495	+                'detection_count': len(detections),
   496	+                'classes': [d['class_name'] for d in detections],
   497	+                'm2_ran': False,
   498	+                'm2_shown_text': m2_res['shown_text'],
   499	+                'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   500	+            })
   501	+            log(f'{rel}: <2 boxes ({len(detections)}), skipped M2 and Gemma')
   502	+            continue
   503	+
   504	+        m2_1 = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
   505	+        m2_2 = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
   506	+        m2_same = rows_identical(m2_1['rows'], m2_2['rows'])
   507	+        used_dets = m2_1['used_detections']
   508	+
   509	+        prompts = build_variant_prompts(used_dets, m2_1['shown_text'])
   510	+        reply_y_only_1, ms_yo_1 = call_gemma(prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   511	+        reply_y_only_2, ms_yo_2 = call_gemma(prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   512	+        reply_y_m2_1, ms_ym_1 = call_gemma(prompts['Y+M2'])
   513	+        reply_y_m2_2, ms_ym_2 = call_gemma(prompts['Y+M2'])
   514	+
   515	+        by_variant_1 = {'Y-ONLY': reply_y_only_1, 'Y+M2': reply_y_m2_1}
   516	+        by_variant_2 = {'Y-ONLY': reply_y_only_2, 'Y+M2': reply_y_m2_2}
   517	+        same_by_variant = {
   518	+            'Y-ONLY': reply_y_only_1 == reply_y_only_2,
   519	+            'Y+M2': reply_y_m2_1 == reply_y_m2_2,
   520	+        }
   521	+
   522	+        coin = blind_key[rel]
   523	+        replies_xy = {'X': by_variant_1[coin['X']], 'Y': by_variant_1[coin['Y']]}
   524	+        identical_xy = {'X': same_by_variant[coin['X']], 'Y': same_by_variant[coin['Y']]}
   525	+
   526	+        rec = {
   527	+            'photo': rel,
   528	+            'source_sha256': item['source_sha256'],
   529	+            'source_bytes': item['source_bytes'],
   530	+            'frame_file': item['frame_file'],
   531	+            'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
   532	+            'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
   533	+            'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
   534	+            'detection_count': len(detections),
   535	+            'detections': detections,
   536	+            'm2_detections': [{**d, 'm2_index': i} for i, d in enumerate(used_dets)],
   537	+            'm2_ran': True,
   538	+            'm2_boxes_used': m2_1['boxes_used'],
   539	+            'm2_raw_top10': m2_1['rows'],
   540	+            'm2_shown_rows': m2_1['shown_rows'],
   541	+            'm2_shown_text': m2_1['shown_text'],
   542	+            'm2_identical_twice': m2_same,
   543	+            'gemma_prompts': prompts,
   544	+            'replies': replies_xy,
   545	+            'replies_identical_twice': identical_xy,
   546	+            'timings_not_a_measurement': {
   547	+                'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   548	+                'm2_ms_run1': m2_1['infer_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   549	+                'm2_ms_run2': m2_2['infer_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   550	+                'gemma_total_ms_not_a_measurement': ms_yo_1 + ms_yo_2 + ms_ym_1 + ms_ym_2,
   551	+            },
   552	+            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   553	+        }
   554	+        if not m2_same:
   555	+            rec['m2_raw_top10_run2'] = m2_2['rows']
   556	+        if not all(identical_xy.values()):
   557	+            rec['replies_run2'] = {'X': by_variant_2[coin['X']], 'Y': by_variant_2[coin['Y']]}
   558	+
   559	+        photo_records.append(rec)
   560	+        log(
   561	+            f"{rel}: boxes={len(detections)} (used={m2_1['boxes_used']}), "
   562	+            f"chosen={len(m2_1['shown_rows'])}, m2_2x={m2_same}, "
   563	+            f"gemma_2x={all(identical_xy.values())} ({TIMING_LABEL})"
   564	+        )
   565	+
   566	+    sheet_m2_path = out_dir / 'sheet_m2.pdf'
   567	+    sheet_ab_path = out_dir / 'sheet_ab.pdf'
   568	+    build_sheet_m2_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, sheet_m2_path)
   569	+    build_sheet_ab_pdf(photo_records, frames_by_photo, sheet_ab_path)
   570	+    log(f'Built PDFs: {sheet_m2_path.name} ({sheet_m2_path.stat().st_size} B), {sheet_ab_path.name} ({sheet_ab_path.stat().st_size} B)')
   571	+
   572	+    end_utc = utc_now()
   573	+    elapsed_s = time.perf_counter() - t0
   574	+
   575	+    qualifying_records = [r for r in photo_records if r['m2_ran']]
   576	+    counts = {
   577	+        'total_discovered': len(all_paths),
   578	+        'processed': len(photo_records),
   579	+        'ge_2_boxes': len(qualifying_records),
   580	+        'lt_2_boxes': sum(1 for r in photo_records if not r['m2_ran']),
   581	+        'gt_32_boxes': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['detection_count'] > relate.MAX_BOXES),
   582	+        'm2_identical_twice': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['m2_identical_twice']),
   583	+        'gemma_x_identical_twice': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['replies_identical_twice']['X']),
   584	+        'gemma_y_identical_twice': sum(1 for r in qualifying_records if r['replies_identical_twice']['Y']),
   585	+        'gemma_both_identical_twice': sum(
   586	+            1 for r in qualifying_records if all(r['replies_identical_twice'].values())
   587	+        ),
   588	+        'failed': len(failed_photos),
   589	+    }
   590	+
   591	+    results_payload = {
   592	+        'task': 'M2-Q1',
   593	+        'accuracy_label': ACCURACY_LABEL,
   594	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   595	+        'instruction': INSTRUCTION,
   596	+        'counts': counts,
   597	+        'failed_photos': failed_photos,
   598	+        'photos': photo_records,
   599	+    }
   600	+    write_json(out_dir / 'results.json', results_payload)
   601	+    write_json(out_dir / 'key.json', blind_key)
   602	+
   603	+    imported_files = (
   604	+        'benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py',
   605	+        'relate.py',
   606	+        'detect_person.py',
   607	+        'picker.py',
   608	+        'chat.py',
   609	+        'server_manager.py',
   610	+    )
   611	+    manifest = {
   612	+        'task': 'M2-Q1',
   613	+        'accuracy_label': ACCURACY_LABEL,
   614	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   615	+        'base_commit': git_base_commit(ROBOT),
   616	+        'start_utc': start_utc,
   617	+        'end_utc': end_utc,
   618	+        'elapsed_s_not_a_measurement': elapsed_s,
   619	+        'limit': limit,
   620	+        'code_sha256': {rel_p: relate.sha256(ROBOT / rel_p) for rel_p in imported_files},
   621	+        'm2_files': {
   622	+            'directory': m2_dir,
   623	+            'sha256': {name: relate.sha256(os.path.join(m2_dir, name)) for name in sorted(relate.HASHES)},
   624	+        },
   625	+        'yolo_files': {
   626	+            str(size): {'path': path, 'sha256': relate.sha256(path)}
   627	+            for size, path in sorted(detect_person.MODELS.items())
   628	+        },
   629	+        'gguf': {
   630	+            'path': server_info['model_path'],
   631	+            'sha256': server_info['gguf_sha256'],
   632	+        },
   633	+        'server_props': server_info['props_summary'],
   634	+        'interpreter': {
   635	+            'executable': sys.executable,
   636	+            'python_version': sys.version.split()[0],
   637	+            'platform': sys.platform,
   638	+            'onnxruntime_version': ort.__version__,
   639	+            'numpy_version': np.__version__,
   640	+            'pil_version': Image.__version__,
   641	+        },
   642	+        'counts': counts,
   643	+        'failed_photos': failed_photos,
   644	+        'processed_photos': [
   645	+            {
   646	+                'photo': r['photo'],
   647	+                'source_sha256': r['source_sha256'],
   648	+                'source_bytes': r['source_bytes'],
   649	+                'frame_file': r['frame_file'],
   650	+                'frame_sha256': r['frame_sha256'],
   651	+                'frame_bytes': r['frame_bytes'],
   652	+                'frame_size': r['frame_size'],
   653	+            }
   654	+            for r in photo_records
   655	+        ],
   656	+        'all_discovered_photos': all_photo_meta,
   657	+    }
   658	+    write_json(out_dir / 'manifest.json', manifest)
   659	+    log(f'DONE M2-Q1 harness in {elapsed_s:.2f}s ({TIMING_LABEL}) -> {out_dir}')
   660	+    return out_dir
   661	+
   662	+
   663	+def main(argv=None):
   664	+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   665	+    parser.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=None, help='Process only the first N sorted photos (smoke run)')
   666	+    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path, default=None, help='Output directory (default: runs/<UTC>)')
   667	+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   668	+    if args.limit is not None and args.limit < 1:
   669	+        parser.error('--limit must be >= 1')
   670	+    run_harness(limit=args.limit, out_dir=args.out)
   671	+
   672	+
   673	+if __name__ == '__main__':
   674	+    main()

   681	+"""Offline unit tests for benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py.
   682	+
   683	+Uses fakes only: no network requests, no ONNX sessions, no GGUF models loaded.
   684	+"""
   685	+from pathlib import Path
   686	+import sys
   687	+import tempfile
   688	+import unittest
   689	+
   690	+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
   691	+
   692	+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
   693	+ROBOT = HERE.parents[2]
   694	+for p in (str(HERE), str(ROBOT)):
   695	+    if p not in sys.path:
   696	+        sys.path.insert(0, p)
   697	+
   698	+from PIL import Image
   699	+
   700	+import quality1
   701	+import relate
   702	+
   703	+
   704	+class FakeHead:
   705	+    def __init__(self, rows=None):
   706	+        self.calls = []
   707	+        self.rows = rows if rows is not None else [
   708	+            {
   709	+                'subject_idx': 0,
   710	+                'subject': 'person',
   711	+                'predicate': 'near',
   712	+                'object_idx': 1,
   713	+                'object': 'chair',
   714	+                'score': 0.82,
   715	+                'threshold': 0.40,
   716	+                'pass': True,
   717	+            },
   718	+            {
   719	+                'subject_idx': 1,
   720	+                'subject': 'chair',
   721	+                'predicate': 'on',
   722	+                'object_idx': 0,
   723	+                'object': 'person',
   724	+                'score': 0.21,
   725	+                'threshold': 0.45,
   726	+                'pass': False,
   727	+            },
   728	+        ]
   729	+
   730	+    def infer(self, image, detections):
   731	+        self.calls.append((image.size, list(detections)))
   732	+        return [dict(r) for r in self.rows]
   733	+
   734	+
   735	+class Quality1Test(unittest.TestCase):
   736	+    def test_xy_assignment_reproducible_and_order_independent(self):
   737	+        photos_a = ['test_photos/b.jpg', 'bench_photos/a.jpg', 'bench_photos/c.jpg', 'test_photos/d.jpg']
   738	+        photos_b = list(reversed(photos_a))
   739	+        key1 = quality1.assign_blind_key(photos_a)
   740	+        key2 = quality1.assign_blind_key(photos_b)
   741	+        self.assertEqual(key1, key2)
   742	+        self.assertEqual(list(key1.keys()), sorted(photos_a))
   743	+        for mapping in key1.values():
   744	+            self.assertEqual(set(mapping.keys()), {'X', 'Y'})
   745	+            self.assertEqual(set(mapping.values()), {'Y-ONLY', 'Y+M2'})
   746	+
   747	+    def test_too_few_handling(self):
   748	+        img = Image.new('RGB', (640, 480), 'gray')
   749	+        for count in (0, 1):
   750	+            head = FakeHead()
   751	+            dets = [
   752	+                {'class_name': 'person', 'conf': 0.9, 'box_xyxy': [10, 10, 100, 200],
   753	+                 'position': 'left', 'distance': 'close'}
   754	+            ][:count]
   755	+            res = quality1.run_m2_once(head, img, dets)
   756	+            self.assertFalse(res['ran'])
   757	+            self.assertEqual(head.calls, [])
   758	+            self.assertEqual(res['boxes_used'], count)
   759	+            self.assertEqual(res['rows'], [])
   760	+            self.assertEqual(res['shown_rows'], [])
   761	+            self.assertEqual(res['shown_text'], relate.TOO_FEW)
   762	+
   763	+    def test_2_to_32_and_over_32_rule_matches_relate(self):
   764	+        img = Image.new('RGB', (640, 480), 'gray')
   765	+        head = FakeHead()
   766	+        # 40 detections with mixed confidence values; must keep top 32 in original detector order.
   767	+        confs = [(i * 17) % 40 / 40.0 + 0.01 for i in range(40)]
   768	+        dets = [
   769	+            {
   770	+                'id': i,
   771	+                'class_name': f'obj_{i}',
   772	+                'conf': confs[i],
   773	+                'box_xyxy': [float(i), float(i), float(i + 20), float(i + 20)],
   774	+                'position': 'center',
   775	+                'distance': 'far',
   776	+            }
   777	+            for i in range(40)
   778	+        ]
   779	+        res = quality1.run_m2_once(head, img, dets)
   780	+        self.assertTrue(res['ran'])
   781	+        self.assertEqual(res['boxes_used'], 32)
   782	+        self.assertEqual(len(head.calls), 1)
   783	+        passed_dets = head.calls[0][1]
   784	+        self.assertEqual(len(passed_dets), 32)
   785	+        # Check detector order is preserved and matches relate.py's exact formula
   786	+        expected_keep = sorted(sorted(range(len(dets)), key=lambda i: -dets[i]['conf'])[:relate.MAX_BOXES])
   787	+        self.assertEqual([d['id'] for d in passed_dets], expected_keep)
   788	+        self.assertEqual(res['shown_rows'], relate.chosen(res['rows']))
   789	+        self.assertEqual(res['shown_text'], relate.context(res['rows']))
   790	+
   791	+    def test_prompts_call_gemma_and_strip_tokens(self):
   792	+        used = [
   793	+            {'class_name': 'person', 'conf': 0.91, 'box_xyxy': [0, 0, 50, 50], 'position': 'left', 'distance': 'close'},
   794	+            {'class_name': 'chair', 'conf': 0.65, 'box_xyxy': [60, 60, 120, 120], 'position': 'right', 'distance': 'medium'},
   795	+        ]
   796	+        m2_text = 'M2 relations:\n- person [0] near chair [1].'
   797	+        prompts = quality1.build_variant_prompts(used, m2_text)
   798	+        self.assertIn(quality1.INSTRUCTION, prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   799	+        self.assertIn('- person [0], left, close\n- chair [1], right, medium', prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   800	+        self.assertNotIn('M2 relations:', prompts['Y-ONLY'])
   801	+        self.assertIn(m2_text, prompts['Y+M2'])
   802	+        self.assertTrue(prompts['Y-ONLY'].startswith('<start_of_turn>user\n'))
   803	+        self.assertTrue(prompts['Y-ONLY'].endswith('<end_of_turn>\n<start_of_turn>model\n'))
   804	+
   805	+        captured = []
   806	+        def fake_post(path, body):
   807	+            captured.append((path, body))
   808	+            return {'content': ' A person is on the left near a chair.<start_of_of_turn></start_of_turn><end_of_turn><start_of_turn'}
   809	+
   810	+        reply, _ = quality1.call_gemma(prompts['Y+M2'], post=fake_post)
   811	+        self.assertEqual(reply, 'A person is on the left near a chair.')
   812	+        self.assertEqual(len(captured), 1)
   813	+        path, payload = captured[0]
   814	+        self.assertEqual(path, '/completion')
   815	+        self.assertEqual(payload['temperature'], 0)
   816	+        self.assertEqual(payload['top_k'], 1)
   817	+        self.assertEqual(payload['n_predict'], 96)
   818	+        self.assertIs(payload['cache_prompt'], False)
   819	+        self.assertEqual(payload['seed'], 0)
   820	+
   821	+    def test_pdf_builders_and_key_never_leaked_into_sheets(self):

   864	+                    'm2_ran': False,
   865	+                    'm2_shown_text': relate.TOO_FEW,
   866	+                },
   867	+            ]
   868	+            m2_pdf = tmp_dir / 'sheet_m2.pdf'
   869	+            ab_pdf = tmp_dir / 'sheet_ab.pdf'
   870	+            quality1.build_sheet_m2_pdf(records, frames, m2_pdf)
   871	+            quality1.build_sheet_ab_pdf(records, frames, ab_pdf)

   876	+            ab_bytes = ab_pdf.read_bytes()
   877	+            self.assertTrue(m2_bytes.startswith(b'%PDF-'))
   878	+            self.assertTrue(ab_bytes.startswith(b'%PDF-'))
   879	+            self.assertLess(len(m2_bytes), 20 * 1024 * 1024)
   880	+            self.assertLess(len(ab_bytes), 20 * 1024 * 1024)
   881	+            # sheet_m2 has 1 qualifying page + 1 summary page = 2 pages; sheet_ab has 1 qualifying page.
   882	+            self.assertIn(b'/Count 2', m2_bytes)
   883	+            self.assertIn(b'/Count 1', ab_bytes)
   884	+            for forbidden in (b'Y-ONLY', b'Y+M2', b'key.json'):
   885	+                self.assertNotIn(forbidden, m2_bytes)
   886	+                self.assertNotIn(forbidden, ab_bytes)
   887	+
   888	+            # Verify build_sheet_ab_pdf refuses any leaked variant name in replies.
   889	+            bad_records = [dict(records[0], replies={'X': 'Variant Y-ONLY reply', 'Y': 'ok'})]
   890	+            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
   891	+                quality1.build_sheet_ab_pdf(bad_records, frames, tmp_dir / 'bad.pdf')

   893	+    def test_prepare_frame_and_server_check_fakes(self):
   894	+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   895	+            tmp_dir = Path(tmp)
   896	+            src = tmp_dir / 'src.jpg'
   897	+            dst = tmp_dir / 'dst.jpg'
   898	+            Image.new('RGB', (1200, 900), (10, 20, 30)).save(src, format='JPEG', quality=95)
   899	+            reopened = quality1.prepare_frame(src, dst)
   900	+            self.assertEqual(reopened.size, (640, 480))
   901	+            self.assertTrue(dst.is_file())
   902	+
   903	+        def ok_get(path):
   904	+            if path == '/health':
   905	+                return {'status': 'ok'}
   906	+            return {
   907	+                'model_path': '/data/data/com.termux/files/home/models/gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf',
   908	+                'model_ftype': 'Q4_0',
   909	+                'total_slots': 1,
   910	+                'default_generation_settings': {'n_ctx': 2048},
   911	+            }
   912	+
   913	+        info = quality1.check_server(get=ok_get)
   914	+        self.assertTrue(info['model_path'].endswith('gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf'))
   915	+
   916	+        def wrong_model_get(path):
   917	+            if path == '/health':
   918	+                return {'status': 'ok'}
   919	+            return {'model_path': '/models/other.gguf'}
   920	+
   921	+        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as ctx:
   922	+            quality1.check_server(get=wrong_model_get)
   923	+        self.assertIn('python3 server_manager.py setup_q4', str(ctx.exception))

   934	+# RelateAnything M2 Quality & Gemma A/B Desk Test (`M2-Q1`)
   935	+
   936	+**Status labels:**
   937	+- **Accuracy VALID (temperature 0); timings NOT measurements (agent resident); grading pending (Local AI, blind).**
   938	+- Motors stay off. No robot files are modified.
   939	+
   940	+## Purpose
   941	+
   942	+Evaluates on every `*.jpg` in `bench_photos/` and `test_photos/` (shrunk to 640 long side, saved at JPEG quality 85, and re-opened):
   943	+1. Whether RelateAnything M2 (`relsgg-vits16`, fp32, via `relate.Head` with SHA-256 verification and without thread pinning) produces accurate object-relation lines (`sheet_m2.pdf`).
   944	+2. Whether adding M2 relation lines (`Y+M2`) helps Gemma 4 E2B Q4_0 (`setup_q4`) describe a scene better than YOLO detections alone (`Y-ONLY`), presented blind as `X` and `Y` (`sheet_ab.pdf`).
   945	+
   946	+Grading is performed blind by Local AI from `sheet_m2.pdf` and `sheet_ab.pdf`. The harness does not grade replies.
   947	+
   948	+## Prerequisites
   949	+
   950	+1. Ensure the robot default server (`Gemma 4 E2B Q4_0`) is running on `127.0.0.1:8080` in native Termux:
   951	+   ```bash
   952	+   cd ~/robot && python3 server_manager.py setup_q4
   953	+   ```
   954	+2. Use the Termux Python interpreter (`/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3`), which provides `onnxruntime`, `numpy`, and `PIL`.
   955	+
   956	+## How to Run
   957	+
   958	+Run offline unit tests (fakes only, no network or model files required):
   959	+```bash
   960	+/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/test_quality1.py -v
   961	+```
   962	+
   963	+Smoke run (first 3 sorted photos):
   964	+```bash
   965	+/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py --limit 3
   966	+```
   967	+
   968	+Full run (all sorted photos in `bench_photos/` and `test_photos/`):
   969	+```bash
   970	+/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py
   971	+```
   972	+
   973	+## Outputs (`runs/<UTC>/`)
   974	+
   975	+- `frames/` — 640-long-side JPEG (quality 85) frames used by YOLO and M2.
   976	+- `results.json` — per-photo hashes, detections, M2 raw top-10 rows and shown text, 2× determinism flags, Gemma prompts, and blind replies `X` and `Y`.
   977	+- `key.json` — blind mapping `photo -> {"X": "Y-ONLY"|"Y+M2", "Y": ...}` generated with `random.Random(20261017)` (never included in PDFs or reports).
   978	+- `sheet_m2.pdf` — one page per `>= 2`-box photo showing the numbered frame, shown M2 text, and raw top-10 M2 rows, plus one summary page listing `< 2`-box photos.
   979	+- `sheet_ab.pdf` — one page per `>= 2`-box photo showing the numbered frame and only the blind replies `X` and `Y`.
   980	+- `manifest.json` — base commit, file SHA-256 hashes, model/GGUF hashes, `/props` summary, interpreter versions, photo hashes, and UTC timestamps.
   981	+- `run.log` — run progress log.

     1	import sys, os, time, queue, threading
     2	import numpy as np
     3	import onnxruntime as ort
     4	from PIL import Image, ImageOps
     5	
     6	CLASSES = ['person','bicycle','car','motorbike','aeroplane','bus','train','truck',
     7	'boat','traffic light','fire hydrant','stop sign','parking meter','bench','bird',
     8	'cat','dog','horse','sheep','cow','elephant','bear','zebra','giraffe','backpack',
     9	'umbrella','handbag','tie','suitcase','frisbee','skis','snowboard','sports ball',
    10	'kite','baseball bat','baseball glove','skateboard','surfboard','tennis racket',
    11	'bottle','wine glass','cup','fork','knife','spoon','bowl','banana','apple',
    12	'sandwich','orange','broccoli','carrot','hot dog','pizza','donut','cake','chair',
    13	'couch','potted plant','bed','dining table','toilet','tv','laptop','mouse','remote',
    14	'keyboard','cell phone','microwave','oven','toaster','sink','refrigerator','book',
    15	'clock','vase','scissors','teddy bear','hair dryer','toothbrush']
    16	
    17	MODELS = {320: os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'yolo11s_320.onnx'),
    18	          640: os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'yolo11s_640.onnx')}
    19	CONF  = 0.35
    20	IOU   = 0.45
    21	
    22	class Detector:
    23	    def __init__(self):
    24	        self.sessions = {size: ort.InferenceSession(path) for size, path in MODELS.items()}
    25	
    26	    def detect(self, frame, size=320):
    27	        if size not in self.sessions:
    28	            raise ValueError('size must be 320 or 640')
    29	        if not isinstance(frame, Image.Image):
    30	            with Image.open(frame) as source:
    31	                frame = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert('RGB')
    32	        width, height = frame.size
    33	        img = frame.resize((size, size)).convert('RGB')
    34	        arr = np.array(img).astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    35	        arr = arr.transpose(2, 0, 1)[np.newaxis]
    36	        session = self.sessions[size]
    37	        out = session.run(None, {session.get_inputs()[0].name: arr})[0][0].T
    38	        by_class = {}
    39	        for pred in out:
    40	            scores = pred[4:]
    41	            cls = int(np.argmax(scores))
    42	            conf = float(scores[cls])
    43	            if conf > CONF:
    44	                cx, cy, w, h = map(float, pred[:4])
    45	                box = (cx-w/2, cy-h/2, cx+w/2, cy+h/2)
    46	                by_class.setdefault(cls, []).append((box, conf))
    47	        results = []
    48	        for cls, items in by_class.items():
    49	            for i in nms([v[0] for v in items], [v[1] for v in items], IOU):
    50	                box, conf = items[i]
    51	                x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    52	                results.append({'class_name': CLASSES[cls], 'class_id': cls,
    53	                                'conf': conf,
    54	                                'box_xyxy': (max(0, min(width, x1*width/size)),
    55	                                             max(0, min(height, y1*height/size)),
    56	                                             max(0, min(width, x2*width/size)),
    57	                                             max(0, min(height, y2*height/size))),
    58	                                'size_used': size})
    59	        return sorted(results, key=lambda d: -d['conf'])
    60	
    61	ROBOT_CPUS = {4, 5}       # MID cores (DECISIONS #128 power map)
    62	ROBOT_INTRA_THREADS = 2   # per session; ORT counts the calling thread as one of them
    63	
    64	
    65	# Copied from benchmark/power_map/power_map.py: thread_ids() (os.listdir instead of pathlib).
    66	def thread_ids():
    67	    return {int(t) for t in os.listdir(f'/proc/{os.getpid()}/task')}
    68	
    69	
    70	# Copied from benchmark/power_map/power_map.py: the set + verify lines of PinnedDetector.loop()/build_detector();
    71	# the tid 0 / main-thread refusal is added.
    72	def pin(tid, cpus):
    73	    if tid in (0, os.getpid()):  # 0 = the caller, pid = the main thread: never pinned
    74	        raise RuntimeError(f'refusing to pin tid {tid}')
    75	    os.sched_setaffinity(tid, cpus)
    76	    if os.sched_getaffinity(tid) != cpus:
    77	        raise RuntimeError(f'thread {tid} affinity did not take effect')
    78	
    79	
    80	class PinnedDetector:
    81	    """The robot's detector (#128): both yolo11s sessions with 2 intra-op / 1 inter-op threads; the 2 new ORT
    82	    workers and one detector-only calling thread are pinned to ROBOT_CPUS and verified. Only these 3 threads are
    83	    ever pinned: never the process, pid 0, the main thread or any other thread.
    84	    Copied from benchmark/power_map/power_map.py: build_detector() ('mid' branch) and PinnedDetector. Changed:
    85	    raises on any failure (no 'default' branch); each request carries its own reply queue, so a late answer can
    86	    never reach a later request; check_affinity()/affinity()/config() added for the robot's trace; no close()
    87	    (daemon thread, ends with the process)."""
    88	
    89	    def __init__(self, cpus=ROBOT_CPUS):
    90	        self.cpus = set(cpus)
    91	        before = thread_ids()  # the diff sees only ORT workers: no other code starts threads meanwhile
    92	        opts = ort.SessionOptions()
    93	        opts.intra_op_num_threads = ROBOT_INTRA_THREADS
    94	        opts.inter_op_num_threads = 1
    95	        self.detector = Detector.__new__(Detector)
    96	        self.detector.sessions = {size: ort.InferenceSession(path, sess_options=opts)
    97	                                  for size, path in MODELS.items()}
    98	        self.worker_tids = sorted(thread_ids() - before)
    99	        expected = len(MODELS) * (ROBOT_INTRA_THREADS - 1)
   100	        if len(self.worker_tids) != expected:
   101	            raise RuntimeError(f'cannot identify ORT workers: expected {expected}, got {self.worker_tids}')
   102	        for tid in self.worker_tids:
   103	            pin(tid, self.cpus)
   104	        self.requests, ready = queue.Queue(), queue.Queue()
   105	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.loop, args=(ready,), daemon=True)
   106	        self.thread.start()
   107	        ok, result = ready.get()
   108	        if not ok:
   109	            self.thread.join()
   110	            raise result
   111	        self.caller_tid = result
   112	
   113	    def loop(self, ready):
   114	        try:
   115	            tid = threading.get_native_id()
   116	            pin(tid, self.cpus)
   117	            ready.put((True, tid))
   118	        except BaseException as e:
   119	            ready.put((False, e))
   120	            return
   121	        while True:
   122	            frame, size, reply = self.requests.get()
   123	            try:
   124	                reply.put((True, self.detector.detect(frame, size)))
   125	            except BaseException as e:
   126	                reply.put((False, e))
   127	
   128	    def detect(self, frame, size=320):
   129	        reply = queue.Queue()
   130	        self.requests.put((frame, size, reply))
   131	        ok, result = reply.get()
   132	        if not ok:
   133	            raise result
   134	        return result
   135	
   136	    def affinity(self):
   137	        """{tid: sorted cpus or error text} for the caller and workers; read only."""
   138	        live, out = thread_ids(), {}
   139	        for tid in [self.caller_tid] + self.worker_tids:
   140	            try:
   141	                if tid not in live:  # never touch a tid that left this process (it could be reused elsewhere)
   142	                    raise ProcessLookupError(f'thread {tid} is gone')
   143	                out[tid] = sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(tid))
   144	            except OSError as e:
   145	                out[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
   146	        return out
   147	
   148	    def check_affinity(self):
   149	        """Re-pin and verify any of the 3 threads whose affinity drifted (Android cpuset changes can reset it).
   150	        Never raises: the result says ok or not."""
   151	        before = self.affinity()
   152	        repin = [tid for tid, cpus in before.items() if isinstance(cpus, list) and set(cpus) != self.cpus]
   153	        errors = {tid: cpus for tid, cpus in before.items() if isinstance(cpus, str)}
   154	        for tid in repin:
   155	            try:
   156	                pin(tid, self.cpus)
   157	            except (OSError, RuntimeError) as e:
   158	                errors[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
   159	        after = self.affinity() if repin else before
   160	        return {'before': before, 'repinned': repin, 'after': after, 'ok': not errors, 'errors': errors}
   161	
   162	    def config(self):
   163	        return {'ort_version': ort.__version__,
   164	                'sessions': {size: {'intra_op_num_threads': s.get_session_options().intra_op_num_threads,
   165	                                    'inter_op_num_threads': s.get_session_options().inter_op_num_threads}
   166	                             for size, s in self.detector.sessions.items()},
   167	                'worker_tids': self.worker_tids, 'caller_tid': self.caller_tid,
   168	                'requested_cpus': sorted(self.cpus), 'affinity': self.affinity()}
   169	
   170	
   171	_detector = None
   172	
   173	def get_detector(pinned=False):
   174	    """Shared detector. pinned=True (the robot process) creates the PinnedDetector; later get_detector() calls,
   175	    e.g. from detect_scene(), then return that same one. Without pinned it is a plain Detector(), as before."""
   176	    global _detector
   177	    if _detector is None:
   178	        _detector = PinnedDetector() if pinned else Detector()
   179	    elif pinned and not isinstance(_detector, PinnedDetector):
   180	        raise RuntimeError('a default detector is already loaded; the robot needs one pinned session pair')
   181	    return _detector
   182	
   183	def iou(a, b):
   184	    ax1,ay1,ax2,ay2 = a
   185	    bx1,by1,bx2,by2 = b
   186	    ix1,iy1 = max(ax1,bx1), max(ay1,by1)
   187	    ix2,iy2 = min(ax2,bx2), min(ay2,by2)
   188	    inter = max(0,ix2-ix1)*max(0,iy2-iy1)
   189	    ua = (ax2-ax1)*(ay2-ay1)+(bx2-bx1)*(by2-by1)-inter
   190	    return inter/ua if ua>0 else 0
   191	
   192	def nms(boxes, confs, iou_thresh=0.45):
   193	    order = sorted(range(len(confs)), key=lambda i: -confs[i])
   194	    keep = []
   195	    while order:
   196	        i = order.pop(0)
   197	        keep.append(i)
   198	        order = [j for j in order if iou(boxes[i], boxes[j]) < iou_thresh]
   199	    return keep
   200	
   201	def scene_from_detections(detections, frame_width, frame_height):
   202	    """Legacy tuple view used by navigation, stereo and text callers."""
   203	    results = []
   204	    seen = set()
   205	    for d in detections:
   206	        label = d['class_name']
   207	        if label in seen:
   208	            continue
   209	        seen.add(label)
   210	        x1, y1, x2, y2 = d['box_xyxy']
   211	        cx, cy, w, h = (x1+x2)/2, (y1+y2)/2, x2-x1, y2-y1
   212	        area = w*h/(frame_width*frame_height)
   213	        pos = 'left' if cx < frame_width/3 else 'right' if cx > 2*frame_width/3 else 'center'
   214	        dist = 'very close' if area>0.3 else 'close' if area>0.1 else 'medium' if area>0.03 else 'far'
   215	        results.append((label, round(d['conf'], 2), pos, dist,
   216	                        round(cx*640/frame_width), round(cy*640/frame_height),
   217	                        round(w*640/frame_width), round(h*640/frame_height)))
   218	    return results
   219	
   220	def detect_scene(image_path, size=640):
   221	    """
   222	    Detect all objects in the scene.
   223	    Returns list of (label, confidence, position, distance)
   224	    """
   225	    with Image.open(image_path) as source:
   226	        frame = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert('RGB')
   227	    return scene_from_detections(get_detector().detect(frame, size), *frame.size)
   228	
   229	def detect_person(image_path):
   230	    """
   231	    Legacy function — returns (found, confidence, position)
   232	    for backwards compatibility with existing code.
   233	    """
   234	    results = detect_scene(image_path)
   235	    for r in results:
   236	        label, conf, pos, dist = r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]
   237	        if label == 'person':
   238	            return True, conf, pos
   239	    return False, 0.0, 'none'
   240	
   241	def scene_to_text(results, coords=False):
   242	    """
   243	    Convert detection results to a natural language sentence.
   244	    coords=True adds pixel coordinates for stereo vision.
   245	    """
   246	    if not results:
   247	        return "empty room, nothing detected"
   248	    if coords:
   249	        parts = [f"{r[0]} x={r[4]} y={r[5]} w={r[6]} h={r[7]} {r[2]} {r[3]}" for r in results]
   250	    else:
   251	        parts = [f"{r[0]} {r[2]}" for r in results]
   252	    return ', '.join(parts)
   253	
   254	def person_direction(results):
   255	    """
   256	    Return direction to move toward detected person.
   257	    Returns 'LEFT', 'RIGHT', 'FORWARD', or None
   258	    """
   259	    for r in results:
   260	        label, conf, pos, dist = r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]
   261	        if label == 'person':
   262	            if pos == 'left':   return 'LEFT'
   263	            if pos == 'right':  return 'RIGHT'
   264	            if pos == 'center': return 'FORWARD'
   265	    return None
   266	
   267	if __name__ == '__main__':
   268	    path = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'test_photos/scene_test.jpg'
   269	    t0 = time.time()
   270	    results = detect_scene(path)
   271	    elapsed = round(time.time()-t0, 3)
   272	    print(f'Detected in {elapsed}s (640-space legacy coordinates):')
   273	    print(f'  {"object":<15} {"conf":<5} {"pos":<8} {"dist":<12} {"cx":>5} {"cy":>5} {"w":>5} {"h":>5}')
   274	    print('  ' + '-'*60)
   275	    for r in results:
   276	        print(f'  {r[0]:<15} {r[1]:<5} {r[2]:<8} {r[3]:<12} {r[4]:>5} {r[5]:>5} {r[6]:>5} {r[7]:>5}')
   277	    print(f'\nScene: "{scene_to_text(results)}"')
   278	    print(f'Coords: "{scene_to_text(results, coords=True)}"')
   279	    direction = person_direction(results)
   280	    if direction:
   281	        print(f"Person direction: {direction}")
   282	    else:
   283	        print("No person detected")

     1	"""Gemma picker (DECISIONS #109, #116, #125, #131): Gemma chooses one behaviour mode by letter scoring; Python
     2	computes which modes are eligible and keeps every motor decision (#104). Gemma never outputs a motor command.
     3

    17	  permit persons to whom the Software is furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:

    20	  the Software.

    23	  WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS OR
    24	  COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR
    25	  OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.

    27	import json
    28	import math
    29	import queue
    30	import threading
    31	import time
    32	import urllib.request
    33	
    34	URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:8080'   # setup_q4's llama-server (the owner starts it before the mission)
    35	TIMEOUT_S = 10                  # per HTTP request
    36	N_PROBS = 1000
    37	MIN_GAP_S = 5                   # an event call starts no sooner than this after the previous call started
    38	HEARTBEAT_S = 20                # otherwise one call every 20 s
    39	INSTRUCTION = 'Choose the single best next action for the robot.'
    40	# Offered in this order; APPROACH and GREET only while a person is in the latest detections.
    41	MODES = {'EXPLORE': 'keep exploring', 'APPROACH': 'go toward the person',
    42	         'GREET': 'stop and greet the person', 'LOOK': 'look around in place'}
    43	PERSON_MODES = ('APPROACH', 'GREET')
    44	LETTERS = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'  # s1/schema.py
    45	MAX_STATE_TOKENS = 1536                                          # s1/schema.py build() default
    46	
    47	
    48	def eligible(person_visible):
    49	    return [m for m in MODES if person_visible or m not in PERSON_MODES]
    50	
    51	
    52	def situation(mission, results, distance, mode, mode_s, last_moves, rooms):
    53	    """The picker's state text without the M2 block (the worker appends it). results: legacy detection tuples."""
    54	    scene = ', '.join(f'{r[0]} ({r[2]}, {r[3]})' for r in results) or 'nothing detected'
    55	    return '\n'.join([
    56	        f'Mission: {mission}',
    57	        f'Scene: {scene}',
    58	        f"Ultrasonic distance ahead: {'no reading' if distance == 999 else f'{distance} cm'}",
    59	        f'Current action: {MODES[mode]} (for {mode_s:.0f} s)',
    60	        f"Last moves: {', '.join(last_moves[-3:]) or 'none'}",
    61	        f"Rooms seen: {', '.join(rooms) or 'none'}"])
    62	
    63	
    64	# Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: post(), with the 10 s timeout; GET when body is None.
    65	def http(path, body=None, url=URL):
    66	    req = urllib.request.Request(url + path, None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(),
    67	                                 {'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    68	    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=TIMEOUT_S) as r:
    69	        return json.loads(r.read())
    70	
    71	
    72	class Gemma:
    73	    """adapters.S1O pointed at setup_q4's server (as coresidency.make_selector). Raises unless /health is ok."""
    74	
    75	    def __init__(self, post=http):
    76	        self.post = post
    77	        self.check = lambda: None  # the Picker sets it: raises Cancelled before any request it must not send
    78	        if post('/health').get('status') != 'ok':
    79	            raise RuntimeError(f'{URL}/health not ok')
    80	        with_bos = post('/tokenize', {'content': '', 'add_special': True})['tokens']
    81	        self.bos_token_id = with_bos[0] if with_bos else None
    82	        self.letter_ids = [self.encode(L)[0] for L in LETTERS[:len(MODES)]]
    83	        assert all(len(self.encode(L)) == 1 for L in LETTERS[:len(MODES)])
    84	        self.prompt_tokens = None
    85	
    86	    def request(self, path, body):
    87	        self.check()
    88	        return self.post(path, body)
    89	
    90	    def encode(self, text):
    91	        return self.request('/tokenize', {'content': text, 'add_special': False})['tokens']
    92	
    93	    def prompt_ids(self, state, phrases):
    94	        """s1/schema.py build() for one 'choice' question, options in the given order, no descriptions."""
    95	        ids = ([self.bos_token_id] if self.bos_token_id is not None else []) + self.encode('<state>\n')
    96	        state_ids = self.encode(state)
    97	        if len(state_ids) > MAX_STATE_TOKENS:  # truncate_tokens(): keep head 70 % and tail
    98	            head = int(MAX_STATE_TOKENS * 0.7)
    99	            tail = MAX_STATE_TOKENS - head - 8
   100	            state_ids = state_ids[:head] + self.encode('\n[... truncated ...]\n') + state_ids[-tail:]
   101	        ids += state_ids + self.encode('\n</state>\n')
   102	        ids += self.encode(''.join([f'\nQuestion (choice): {INSTRUCTION.strip()}', '\nOptions:',
   103	                                    *(f'\n({LETTERS[j]}) {p.strip()}' for j, p in enumerate(phrases)),
   104	                                    '\nAnswer: (']))
   105	        return ids
   106	
   107	    def decide(self, state, phrases):
   108	        """{phrase: probability}: the letter probabilities at the 'Answer: (' slot, renormalised (S1O.decide)."""
   109	        ids = self.prompt_ids(state, phrases)
   110	        res = self.request('/completion', {'prompt': ids, 'n_predict': 1, 'n_probs': N_PROBS, 'post_sampling_probs': False,
   111	                                        'temperature': 0, 'cache_prompt': False})
   112	        by_id = {e['id']: (e['prob'] if 'prob' in e else math.exp(e['logprob']))
   113	                 for e in res['completion_probabilities'][0]['top_logprobs']}
   114	        raw = {}
   115	        for j, phrase in enumerate(phrases):
   116	            p = by_id.get(self.letter_ids[j])
   117	            if p is None:
   118	                raise RuntimeError(f'letter {LETTERS[j]} outside top-{N_PROBS}')
   119	            raw[phrase] = p
   120	        mass = sum(raw.values())
   121	        self.prompt_tokens = len(ids)
   122	        return {k: v / mass for k, v in raw.items()}
   123	
   124	
   125	class Cadence:
   126	    """When to call (owner D2). An event (person appears or disappears; the ultrasonic becomes blocked) calls at
   127	    once, but not sooner than MIN_GAP_S after the previous call started; otherwise every HEARTBEAT_S. An event that
   128	    arrives while a call is not allowed stays pending (the latest one wins). The first call is due at once."""
   129	
   130	    def __init__(self):
   131	        self.last_start = None
   132	        self.person = None
   133	        self.blocked = False
   134	        self.pending = 'start'
   135	
   136	    def due(self, now, person, blocked, ready):
   137	        """The trigger name when a call starts now, else None. ready: False while a call is in flight, at SEVERE or
   138	        without a 640 frame; the observed state is still updated."""
   139	        if self.person is not None and person != self.person:
   140	            self.pending = 'person_appeared' if person else 'person_left'
   141	        if blocked and not self.blocked:
   142	            self.pending = 'blocked'
   143	        self.person, self.blocked = person, blocked
   144	        if not ready:
   145	            return None
   146	        if self.last_start is not None and now - self.last_start < MIN_GAP_S:
   147	            return None
   148	        trigger = self.pending or ('heartbeat' if now - self.last_start >= HEARTBEAT_S else None)
   149	        if trigger:
   150	            self.pending, self.last_start = None, now
   151	        return trigger
   152	
   153	
   154	class Cancelled(Exception):
   155	    """The mission withdrew permission (left ACTIVE, Android status SEVERE, or stop) while a pick was running."""
   156	
   157	
   158	class Picker:
   159	    """One background worker thread runs each pick: M2 (relate.Relate) on the latest 640 frame, then Gemma. It gets
   160	    plain data in a request and returns a result; it never touches motors, camera, heat state or the robot. Any
   161	    failure becomes an EXPLORE result with the reason (a fallback, never a stop). The mission thread calls allow()
   162	    while ACTIVE and below SEVERE and forbid() otherwise; a running pick checks before M2, after M2 and before every
   163	    HTTP request, and a forbid() cancels it for good (a later allow() does not revive it). Only a request already
   164	    sent (<= TIMEOUT_S) or an M2 inference already started can still finish."""
   165	
   166	    def __init__(self, gemma, relate):
   167	        self.gemma, self.relate = gemma, relate
   168	        self.requests, self.results = queue.Queue(), queue.Queue()
   169	        self.allowed = threading.Event()
   170	        self.generation = 0  # forbid() bumps it; a pick runs only while it is unchanged
   171	        ready = queue.Queue()
   172	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.loop, args=(ready,), daemon=True)
   173	        self.thread.start()
   174	        error = ready.get()
   175	        if error:
   176	            self.thread.join()
   177	            raise error
   178	
   179	    def loop(self, ready):
   180	        try:
   181	            self.relate.pin_caller()  # this thread runs M2: BIG 6-7, verified
   182	        except BaseException as e:
   183	            ready.put(e)
   184	            return
   185	        ready.put(None)
   186	        while (request := self.requests.get()) is not None:
   187	            self.results.put(self.pick(request))
   188	
   189	    def allow(self):
   190	        self.allowed.set()
   191	
   192	    def forbid(self):
   193	        if self.allowed.is_set():
   194	            self.generation += 1
   195	            self.allowed.clear()
   196	
   197	    def pick(self, request):
   198	        out = {'slot': request['slot'], 'trigger': request['trigger'],
   199	               'eligible': [MODES[m] for m in request['eligible']], 'mode': 'EXPLORE', 'probs': None,
   200	               'm2_ms': None, 'm2_boxes': None, 'm2_relations': None, 'm2_repinned': None,
   201	               'picker_ms': None, 'prompt_tokens': None, 'fallback': None, 'cancelled': False}
   202	        generation = self.generation
   203	
   204	        def check():
   205	            if generation != self.generation or not self.allowed.is_set():
   206	                raise Cancelled('optional work not allowed (left ACTIVE, SEVERE or stop)')
   207	        self.gemma.check = check
   208	        stage = 'M2'
   209	        try:
   210	            check()
   211	            affinity = self.relate.check_affinity()
   212	            out['m2_repinned'] = affinity['repinned']
   213	            if not affinity['ok']:
   214	                raise RuntimeError(f"affinity: {affinity['errors']}")
   215	            check()  # a forbid() during the affinity check: no M2 run
   216	            text, out['m2_boxes'], out['m2_relations'], out['m2_ms'] = self.relate.relations(*request['frame_640'])
   217	            check()
   218	            stage = 'Gemma'
   219	            phrases = out['eligible']
   220	            started = time.perf_counter()
   221	            dist = self.gemma.decide(request['situation'] + '\n' + text, phrases)
   222	            check()  # a forbid() while /completion was in flight cancels it too
   223	            out['picker_ms'] = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
   224	            out['probs'] = dist
   225	            out['prompt_tokens'] = self.gemma.prompt_tokens
   226	            choice = max(phrases, key=lambda k: dist[k])  # ties: first offered, as in ladder.py
   227	            out['mode'] = request['eligible'][phrases.index(choice)]
   228	        except Exception as e:
   229	            out['mode'] = 'EXPLORE'
   230	            out['fallback'] = f'{stage}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
   231	            out['cancelled'] = isinstance(e, Cancelled)
   232	        return out
   233	
   234	    def submit(self, request):
   235	        self.requests.put(request)
   236	
   237	    def result(self):
   238	        """A finished pick, or None (never waits)."""
   239	        try:
   240	            return self.results.get_nowait()
   241	        except queue.Empty:
   242	            return None
   243	
   244	    def stop(self, timeout=TIMEOUT_S + 5):
   245	        """Cancels the running pick (after its current request) and ends the worker; True when it has ended."""
   246	        self.forbid()
   247	        self.requests.put(None)
   248	        self.thread.join(timeout)
   249	        return not self.thread.is_alive()
   250	
   251	    def config(self):
   252	        return {'url': URL, 'options': list(MODES.values()), 'instruction': INSTRUCTION, 'timeout_s': TIMEOUT_S,
   253	                'cadence': {'events': ['person_appeared', 'person_left', 'blocked'], 'min_gap_s': MIN_GAP_S,
   254	                            'heartbeat_s': HEARTBEAT_S, 'phases': ['ACTIVE'], 'not_at_status': 'SEVERE or above'},
   255	                'm2': {'cpus': sorted(self.relate.cpus), 'threads': 2, 'worker_tids': self.relate.worker_tids,
   256	                       'caller_tid': self.relate.caller_tid, 'affinity': self.relate.affinity(),
   257	                       'parity': 'graph SHA-256 equals the parity-passed graph (#130)'}}
