    15	    ('Qwen3.5 4B Q6_K', 'qwen35/Qwen3.5-4B-Q6_K.gguf', 'qwen', 2048),
    16	    ('Mistral 7B Q4_K_M', 'mistral-7b-instruct-v0.2.Q4_K_M.gguf', 'mistral', 2048),
    17	    ('Gemma 4 E4B Q4_0', 'gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_0.gguf', 'gemma', 4096),
    18	)
    19	available_models = [
    20	    entry for entry in MODEL_FILES
    21	    if (Path(HOME) / 'models' / entry[1]).is_file()
    22	    and (Path(HOME) / 'models' / entry[1]).stat().st_size > 1000
    23	]
    24	MODELS = {
    25	    str(i): {'name': name, 'file': file, 'type': family, 'ctx': ctx}
    26	    for i, (name, file, family, ctx) in enumerate(available_models, 1)
    27	}
    28	
    29	SERVER  = HOME + '/llama.cpp-b1609-dotprod/build/bin/llama-server'
    30	SERVER_LOG = HOME + '/robot-chat-server.log'
    31	URL     = 'http://127.0.0.1:8080/completion'
    32	MTP_MODEL = 'gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf'  # E2B MTP entry (DECISIONS #120)
    33	E4B_MTP_MODEL = 'gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_0.gguf'
    34	current_model = None
    35	mtp_on = False
    36	history = []
    37	
    38	# ── Colors ────────────────────────────────────────────
    39	class C:
    40	    BLUE    = '\033[94m'
    41	    GREEN   = '\033[92m'
    42	    YELLOW  = '\033[93m'
    43	    RED     = '\033[91m'
    44	    CYAN    = '\033[96m'
    45	    BOLD    = '\033[1m'
    46	    DIM     = '\033[2m'
    47	    RESET   = '\033[0m'
    48	
    49	def colored(text, color):
    50	    return f'{color}{text}{C.RESET}'
    51	
    52	# ── Temperature ───────────────────────────────────────
    53	def get_ram():
    54	    try:
    55	        with open('/proc/meminfo') as info:
    56	            kb = next(int(line.split()[1]) for line in info if line.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
    57	        return f'RAM:{kb / 1024 / 1024:.1f}GiB available'
    58	    except (OSError, StopIteration, ValueError):
    59	        return ''
    60	
    61	def get_temp():
    62	    ram = get_ram()
    63	    try:
    64	        t = int(os.popen('su -c "cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/temp"').read().strip()) // 1000
    65	        b = int(os.popen('su -c "cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone25/temp"').read().strip()) // 1000
    66	        cpu_color = C.RED if t > 80 else C.YELLOW if t > 65 else C.GREEN
    67	        return f'CPU:{colored(str(t)+"°C", cpu_color)} Batt:{b}°C {ram}'
    68	    except:
    69	        return ram
    70	
    71	# ── Server ────────────────────────────────────────────
    72	def server_cmd(model_key, draft=False):
    73	    """llama-server argv for a menu entry, without taskset; draft adds the MTP drafter flags.
    74	    benchmark/.../chat_mtp/chat_mtp.py launches this same list."""
    75	    m = MODELS[model_key]
    76	    cmd = [
    77	        SERVER, '-m', f'{HOME}/models/{m["file"]}', '--port', '8080',
    78	        '--ctx-size', str(m['ctx']), '--host', '127.0.0.1',
    79	        '--threads', '4', '--threads-batch', '4', '--parallel', '1',
    80	        '--swa-full', '--cache-ram', '0',
    81	    ]
    82	    draft_args = E4B_MTP_ARGS if m['file'] == E4B_MTP_MODEL else MTP_ARGS
    83	    return cmd + draft_args if draft else cmd
    84	
    85	def kill_server():
    86	    subprocess.run(['pkill', '-f', '^' + SERVER + ' '], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    87	                   stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    88	    time.sleep(1)
    89	    print(colored('  Server killed', C.YELLOW))
    90	
    91	def start_server(model_key):
    92	    global current_model, mtp_on
    93	    m = MODELS[model_key]
    94	    model_path = f'{HOME}/models/{m["file"]}'
    95	
    96	    if not os.path.exists(model_path) or os.path.getsize(model_path) < 1000:
    97	        print(colored(f'  Model file not found or incomplete: {m["file"]}', C.RED))
    98	        return False
    99	
   100	    kill_server()
   101	    time.sleep(1)
   102	    if server_running():
   103	        print(colored('  Port 8080 is still in use by another server', C.RED))
   104	        return False
   105	
   106	    e4b = m['file'] == E4B_MTP_MODEL
   107	    draft = m['file'] in (MTP_MODEL, E4B_MTP_MODEL)
   108	    if draft and not (e4b_mtp_draft_ok() if e4b else mtp_draft_ok()):
   109	        draft_path = E4B_MTP_DRAFT if e4b else MTP_DRAFT
   110	        print(colored(f'  MTP drafter missing or wrong size, starting without it: {draft_path}', C.YELLOW))
   111	        draft = False
   112	    print(colored(f'  Loading {m["name"]}...', C.CYAN))
   113	    cmd = server_cmd(model_key, draft)
   114	    if not shutil.which('taskset'):
   115	        print(colored('  taskset unavailable; using Android CPU scheduling', C.YELLOW))
   116	    elif {4, 5, 6, 7}.issubset(os.sched_getaffinity(0)):
   117	        cmd[:0] = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7']
   118	    else:
   119	        print(colored('  Cores 4-7 unavailable; using Android CPU scheduling', C.YELLOW))
   120	    try:
   121	        with open(SERVER_LOG, 'w') as log:
   122	            server = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   123	                                      start_new_session=True)
   124	    except OSError as e:
   125	        print(colored(f'  Failed to launch server: {e}', C.RED))
   126	        return False
   127	
   128	    # Wait for ready
   129	    import urllib.request
   130	    for i in range(40):
   131	        time.sleep(1)
   132	        if server.poll() is not None:
   133	            break
   134	        try:
   135	            with urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8080/health', timeout=2) as response:
   136	                if response.status != 200:
   137	                    continue
   138	            print(colored(f'  Ready after {i+1}s | MTP {"on" if draft else "off"} | {get_temp()}', C.GREEN))
   139	            current_model = model_key
   140	            mtp_on = draft
   141	            return True
   142	        except:
   143	            if i % 5 == 0:
   144	                print(f'  Starting... {i+1}s')
   145	    if server.poll() is None:
   146	        server.terminate()
   147	        try:
   148	            server.wait(timeout=5)
   149	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   150	            server.kill()
   151	            server.wait()
   152	    print(colored(f'  Failed to start server (exit {server.returncode}); see {SERVER_LOG}', C.RED))
   153	    return False
   154	
   155	def server_running():
   156	    try:
   157	        requests.get('http://127.0.0.1:8080/health', timeout=2)
   158	        return True
   159	    except:
   160	        return False
   161	
   162	# ── Prompt builders ───────────────────────────────────
   163	def build_prompt(model_type, messages):
   164	    if model_type == 'gemma':
   165	        prompt = ''
   166	        for msg in messages:
   167	            role = msg['role']
   168	            content = msg['content']
   169	            if role == 'system':
   170	                prompt += f'<start_of_turn>user\n{content}<end_of_turn>\n<start_of_turn>model\nUnderstood.<end_of_turn>\n'
   171	            elif role == 'user':
   172	                prompt += f'<start_of_turn>user\n{content}<end_of_turn>\n'
   173	            elif role == 'assistant':
   174	                prompt += f'<start_of_turn>model\n{content}<end_of_turn>\n'
   175	        prompt += '<start_of_turn>model\n'
   176	        return prompt
   177	
   178	    elif model_type == 'qwen':
   179	        prompt = ''
   180	        for msg in messages:
   181	            role = msg['role']
   182	            content = msg['content']
   183	            if role == 'system':
   184	                prompt += f'<|im_start|>system\n{content}<|im_end|>\n'
   185	            elif role == 'user':
   186	                prompt += f'<|im_start|>user\n{content}<|im_end|>\n'
   187	            elif role == 'assistant':
   188	                prompt += f'<|im_start|>assistant\n{content}<|im_end|>\n'
   189	        prompt += '<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n'
   190	        return prompt
   191	
   192	    elif model_type == 'mistral':
   193	        prompt = ''
   194	        for msg in messages:
   195	            role = msg['role']
   196	            content = msg['content']
   197	            if role == 'user':
   198	                prompt += f'[INST] {content} [/INST]'
   199	            elif role == 'assistant':
   200	                prompt += f' {content}</s>'
   201	        return prompt
   202	
   203	    return ''
   204	
   205	def get_stop_tokens(model_type):
   206	    stops = {
   207	        'gemma':   ['<end_of_turn>'],
   208	        'qwen':    ['<|im_end|>'],
   209	        'mistral': ['</s>', '[INST]'],
   210	    }
   211	    return stops.get(model_type, [])
   212	
   213	def chat_payload(prompt, model_type):
   214	    return {
   215	        'prompt':      prompt,
   216	        'n_predict':   512,
   217	        'temperature': 0.7,
   218	        'stop':        get_stop_tokens(model_type),
   219	        'stream':      False,
   220	    }
   221	
   222	# ── Chat ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   223	def chat(user_input):
   224	    m = MODELS[current_model]
   225	
   226	    # Add to history
   227	    history.append({'role': 'user', 'content': user_input})
   228	
   229	    # Build prompt
   230	    prompt = build_prompt(m['type'], history)
   231	
   232	    payload = chat_payload(prompt, m['type'])
   233	
   234	    # Call
   235	    t0 = time.time()
   236	    try:
   237	        resp = requests.post(URL, json=payload, timeout=120)
   238	        data = resp.json()
   239	        if 'content' not in data:
   240	            raise ValueError(f"{data.get('error', data)}; use /clear to start a fresh conversation")
   241	        reply = data['content'].strip()
   242	        elapsed = round(time.time()-t0, 1)
   243	        tok_s = round(data['timings']['predicted_per_second'], 1)
   244	
   245	        history.append({'role': 'assistant', 'content': reply})
   246	        return reply, elapsed, tok_s
   247	    except Exception as e:
   248	        history.pop()
   249	        return f'Error: {e}', 0, 0
   250	
   251	# ── UI ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   252	def show_header():
   253	    os.system('clear')
   254	    print(colored('='*50, C.CYAN))
   255	    print(colored('  ROBOT LLM CHAT', C.BOLD + C.CYAN))
   256	    print(colored('='*50, C.CYAN))
   257	    if current_model:
   258	        m = MODELS[current_model]
   259	        print(f'  Model: {colored(m["name"], C.YELLOW)} | MTP {"on" if mtp_on else "off"}')
   260	        print(f'  No vision | {get_temp()}')
   261	    print(colored('='*50, C.CYAN))
   262	
   263	def show_menu():
   264	    show_header()
   265	    print(f'\n  {colored("COMMANDS:", C.BOLD)}')
   266	    print(f'  {colored("/switch", C.CYAN)}   — switch model')
   267	    print(f'  {colored("/clear", C.CYAN)}    — clear conversation')
   268	    print(f'  {colored("/history", C.CYAN)}  — show conversation')
   269	    print(f'  {colored("/temp", C.CYAN)}     — show temperatures')
   270	    print(f'  {colored("/kill", C.CYAN)}     — kill server')
   271	    print(f'  {colored("/quit", C.CYAN)}     — exit')
   272	    print()
   273	
   274	def select_model():
   275	    if not MODELS:
   276	        print(colored('  No supported model files found in ~/models', C.RED))
   277	        return False
   278	    print(f'\n  {colored("AVAILABLE MODELS:", C.BOLD)}')
   279	    for key, m in MODELS.items():
   280	        model_path = f'{HOME}/models/{m["file"]}'
   281	        exists = os.path.exists(model_path) and os.path.getsize(model_path) > 1000
   282	        status = colored('✅', C.GREEN) if exists else colored('❌ missing', C.RED)
   283	        size = f'{round(os.path.getsize(model_path)/1024/1024/1024, 1)}GB' if exists else '?'
   284	        print(f'  {colored(key, C.YELLOW)}) {m["name"]} [{size}] {status}')
   285	
   286	    print(f'\n  Enter number (or ENTER to cancel): ', end='')
   287	    choice = input().strip()
   288	    if choice in MODELS:
   289	        model_path = f'{HOME}/models/{MODELS[choice]["file"]}'
   290	        if not os.path.exists(model_path) or os.path.getsize(model_path) < 1000:
   291	            print(colored('  Model not available', C.RED))
   292	            time.sleep(2)
   293	            return False
   294	        return start_server(choice)
   295	    return None
   296	
   297	# ── Main loop ─────────────────────────────────────────
   298	def main():
   299	    global history
   300	
   301	    show_header()
   302	    print(colored('\n  Welcome! Select a model to start.\n', C.GREEN))
   303	
   304	    if not current_model:
   305	        select_model()
   306	
   307	    if not current_model:
   308	        print(colored('  No model loaded. Exiting.', C.RED))
   309	        return
   310	
   311	    show_menu()
   312	
   313	    while True:
   314	        # Prompt
   315	        model_name = MODELS[current_model]['name'].split('(')[0].strip()
   316	        print(f'\n{colored("You", C.GREEN + C.BOLD)}: ', end='')
   317	
   318	        try:
   319	            user_input = input().strip()
   320	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
   321	            print()
   322	            break
   323	
   324	        if not user_input:
   325	            continue
   326	
   327	        # Commands
   328	        if user_input.startswith('/'):
   329	            cmd = user_input.lower().split()[0]
   330	
   331	            if cmd == '/quit' or cmd == '/exit':
   332	                break
   333	
   334	            elif cmd == '/kill':
   335	                kill_server()
   336	
   337	            elif cmd == '/switch':
   338	                switched = select_model()
   339	                if switched is False:
   340	                    print(colored('  Model switch failed; exiting chat', C.RED))
   341	                    break
   342	                if switched:
   343	                    history = []
   344	                show_menu()
   345	
   346	            elif cmd == '/clear':
   347	                history = []
   348	                print(colored('  Conversation cleared', C.YELLOW))
   349	
   350	            elif cmd == '/history':
   351	                print()
   352	                for msg in history:
   353	                    role_color = C.GREEN if msg['role'] == 'user' else C.CYAN
   354	                    print(colored(f'{msg["role"].upper()}:', role_color))
   355	                    print(f'  {msg["content"][:200]}...' if len(msg['content']) > 200 else f'  {msg["content"]}')
   356	                    print()
   357	
   358	            elif cmd == '/temp':
   359	                print(colored(f'\n  {get_temp()}', C.CYAN))
   360	                # Show all zones
   361	                zones = {'BIG':9,'MID':10,'LITTLE':11,'GPU':12,'Battery':25}
   362	                for name, zone in zones.items():
   363	                    try:
   364	                        t = int(os.popen(f'su -c "cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone{zone}/temp"').read().strip()) // 1000
   365	                        bar = colored('●', C.RED) if t>80 else colored('●', C.YELLOW) if t>65 else colored('●', C.GREEN)
   366	                        print(f'  {bar} {name:<8}: {t}°C')
   367	                    except:
   368	                        pass
   369	
   370	            elif cmd in ('/photo', '/camera'):
   371	                print(colored('  Image chat is unavailable with the current local models', C.RED))
   372	
   373	            else:
   374	                print(colored(f'  Unknown command: {cmd}', C.RED))
   375	
   376	            continue
   377	
   378	        # Check server still running
   379	        if not server_running():
   380	            print(colored('  Server died — restarting...', C.RED))
   381	            if not start_server(current_model):
   382	                break
   383	
   384	        # Chat
   385	        print(f'\n{colored(model_name, C.CYAN + C.BOLD)}: ', end='', flush=True)
   386	        reply, elapsed, tok_s = chat(user_input)
   387	
   388	        print(reply)
   389	        print(colored(f'\n  [{elapsed}s | {tok_s}tok/s | {get_temp()}]', C.DIM))
   390	
   391	    print(colored('\n  Goodbye!', C.CYAN))
   392	    print(colored('  Stop the server from Termux with: pkill -f llama-server', C.DIM))
   393	
   394	if __name__ == '__main__':
   395	    main()

    33	def kill_servers():
    34	    os.system("pkill -f llama-server 2>/dev/null")
    35	    time.sleep(2)
    36	    print("[SERVER] All servers stopped")

    38	def wait_for_server(port=8080, timeout=60):
    39	    for i in range(timeout):
    40	        time.sleep(1)
    41	        try:
    42	            r = urllib.request.urlopen(
    43	                "http://127.0.0.1:" + str(port) + "/health", timeout=2)
    44	            if r.status == 200:
    45	                print("[SERVER] Ready after " + str(i+1) + "s")
    46	                return True
    47	        except:
    48	            pass
    49	    print("[SERVER] ERROR: failed to load in " + str(timeout) + "s")
    50	    return False
    51	
    52	def start_server(model_path, port=8080, ctx=2048, extra_args=""):
    53	    kill_servers()
    54	    cmd = (
    55	        LLAMA_SERVER +
    56	        " -m " + model_path +
    57	        " --port " + str(port) +
    58	        " --ctx-size " + str(ctx) +
    59	        " --threads " + THREADS +
    60	        " --threads-batch " + BATCH_THREADS +
    61	        " --parallel 1" +
    62	        " --swa-full" +
    63	        " --cache-ram 0" +
    64	        " --host 127.0.0.1 " +
    65	        extra_args +
    66	        " 2>/dev/null &"
    67	    )
    68	    os.system(cmd)
    69	    print("[SERVER] Starting: " + model_path.split("/")[-1])
    70	    print("[SERVER] Waiting...")
    71	    return wait_for_server(port)

    73	def start_setup(setup_name):
    74	    print("\n" + "="*50)
    75	    print("  Starting: " + setup_name)
    76	    print("="*50)
    77	
    78	    # ── MAIN ROBOT MODEL ──────────────────────────────
    79	    if setup_name == "setup_q4":
    80	        # E2B Q4_0 — default robot model
    81	        # Speed: ~12 tok/s (dotprod build) | Size: 2.8GB
    82	        return start_server(
    83	            HOME + "/models/gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf",
    84	            port=8080, ctx=2048
    85	        )
    86	
    87	    # ── MAIN ROBOT MODEL + MTP DRAFTER (opt-in) ───────
    88	    elif setup_name == "setup_q4_mtp":
    89	        # setup_q4 plus the DECISIONS #120 drafter flags; not the robot default
    90	        if not mtp_draft_ok():
    91	            print("[SERVER] ERROR: MTP drafter missing or not " + str(MTP_DRAFT_SIZE) +
    92	                  " bytes: " + MTP_DRAFT + "; nothing started")
    93	            return False
    94	        return start_server(
    95	            HOME + "/models/gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf",
    96	            port=8080, ctx=2048, extra_args=" ".join(MTP_ARGS)
    97	        )
    98	
    99	    # ── QUALITY MODE ──────────────────────────────────
   100	    elif setup_name == "setup_e4b":
   101	        # E4B Q4_K_M — smarter but slower
   102	        # Speed: ~5.4 tok/s (dotprod build) | Size: 5.0GB
   103	        return start_server(
   104	            HOME + "/models/gemma-4-e4b-it-q4_k_m.gguf",
   105	            port=8080, ctx=2048
   106	        )
   107	
   108	    # E4B Q4_0: opt-in, not the robot default.
   109	    elif setup_name == "setup_e4b_q4":
   110	        return start_server(
   111	            HOME + "/models/gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_0.gguf",
   112	            port=8080, ctx=2048
   113	        )
   114	
   115	    elif setup_name == "setup_e4b_q4_mtp":
   116	        if not e4b_mtp_draft_ok():
   117	            print("[SERVER] ERROR: MTP drafter missing or not " + str(E4B_MTP_DRAFT_SIZE) +
   118	                  " bytes: " + E4B_MTP_DRAFT + "; nothing started")
   119	            return False
   120	        return start_server(
   121	            HOME + "/models/gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_0.gguf",
   122	            port=8080, ctx=2048, extra_args=" ".join(E4B_MTP_ARGS)
   123	        )
   124	
   125	    # ── STOP ─────────────────────────────────────────
   126	    elif setup_name == "stop":
   127	        kill_servers()
   128	        return True
   129	
   130	    else:
   131	        print("[SERVER] Unknown setup: " + setup_name)
   132	        print("[SERVER] Available: setup_q4, setup_q4_mtp, setup_e4b, stop")
   133	        print("[SERVER] Opt-in: setup_e4b_q4, setup_e4b_q4_mtp")
   134	        return False
   135	
   136	if __name__ == "__main__":
   137	    import sys
   138	    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
   139	        print("Usage: python3 server_manager.py [setup_q4|setup_q4_mtp|setup_e4b|setup_e4b_q4|setup_e4b_q4_mtp|stop]")
   140	        sys.exit(1)
   141	    start_setup(sys.argv[1])

/bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba /root/.codex/plugins/cache/ponytail/ponytail/5.3.2/skills/ponytail-review/SKILL.md' in /data/data/com.termux/files/home/review/m2q1/round1_copy
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	---
     2	name: ponytail-review
     3	description: >
     4	  Quality review of a change: is the logic right, is it safe, does it hold
     5	  under real load, is risky code tested, is it fast enough, and is every line
     6	  needed. Reads the connected code, not only the diff. Each finding is
     7	  explained in plain English. Use for "review this", "code review", "review
     8	  the last commit", "review my PR", "is this over-engineered", /ponytail-review.
     9	---
    10	
    11	Review a change like the senior developer who will be paged when it breaks.
    12	Order of importance: correct, safe, holds under load, tested, fast, lean.
    13	Lean still matters: every extra line must be read, tested and fixed later.
    14	This is a report the user asked for, so give it in full.
    15	
    16	## 1. Understand first
    17	
    18	- Review what the user names: uncommitted or staged changes, a branch, a PR
    19	  link, or files. Nothing named: the uncommitted changes, or the last commit
    20	  if there are none.
    21	- Read the diff, then the code it touches: callers of every changed function,
    22	  the functions it calls, the tests, the README.
    23	- Trace the real flow: where data comes in, what is stored, what goes out.
    24	- A change can break code it does not touch. When a signature, return value
    25	  or behavior changes, grep every caller.
    26	- Find the expected load in the repo (README, deploy config): one person
    27	  running a script, or many users and processes at once. Judge scale against
    28	  that, and say which load you assumed.
    29	
    30	## 2. Look for
    31	
    32	1. **Bug:** wrong result, crash, missed edge case (empty, zero, last item,
    33	   rounding, time zones), a caller broken by the change, a fix applied in one
    34	   caller while the shared function stays broken.
    35	2. **Risk:** security holes (injection, weak randomness, secrets, missing
    36	   checks on input from users), data loss (errors swallowed, writes in the
    37	   wrong order, no transaction).
    38	3. **Scale:** fine for one user, wrong for many: check-then-write races, the
    39	   same work done by every process, memory or lists that only grow, a query
    40	   per item, O(n^2) on big input, per-process state that must be shared.
    41	4. **Missing test:** risky new logic (a branch, a parser, money, security,
    42	   data writes, a bug fix) with no test that fails when it breaks. One good
    43	   test, not coverage.
    44	5. **Speed:** big slowdowns are problems. Small wins (work repeated in a hot
    45	   loop) are suggestions; some software counts every millisecond.
    46	6. **Lean:** code that should not exist or should be smaller.
    47	   - delete: dead code, unused options, speculative features
    48	   - reuse: the repo already has this helper (name the path)
    49	   - stdlib / native: the standard library or platform already does it; a
    50	     new dependency for a few lines
    51	   - yagni: abstraction with one implementation, config nobody sets
    52	   - merge: near-copies that must change together
    53	   - split: one function doing several unrelated jobs, so it is hard to read
    54	     or test. Split by job, never by line count, and never into helpers that
    55	     exist only to make a function shorter.
    56	
    57	## 3. Check before you report
    58	
    59	- Every finding needs a concrete case: "this input or situation leads to this
    60	  wrong result". No case, no finding.
    61	- Re-read the lines and confirm: the caller exists, the value can really be
    62	  empty, the code really is unused.
    63	- A shortcut marked with a `shortcut:` (or older `ponytail:`) comment that names its limit is a
    64	  decision, not a finding, unless the expected load already crosses it.
    65	- Propose the smallest fix that works. Prefer fixes that delete code. Never
    66	  add layers, frameworks or config the problem does not need.
    67	- No style taste, no "consider", no vague worries.
    68	
    69	## 4. Output
    70	
    71	Very simple English: short sentences, everyday words. Explain a technical
    72	term the first time you use it. The reader may never have seen this code.
    73	
    74	Start with `What this change does:` in two or three sentences.
    75	
    76	Then the findings in three groups, skip empty groups:
    77	- **Must fix:** bug, security, data loss, breaks at the expected load.
    78	- **Should fix:** risky code without a test, real slowness, duplication, a
    79	  function that mixes jobs, code that should not exist.
    80	- **Nice to have:** small speed-ups, shorter forms.
    81	
    82	Number findings across all groups, so the user can say "fix 2 and 5".
    83	Every finding has all four parts, each one or two short sentences:
    84	
    85	2. **Orders land on the wrong day** (`billing/close_day.py:L40-52`)
    86	   - **What this is:** At midnight this job closes the day and bills all orders of that day.
    87	   - **Problem:** It takes "today" from the server clock, which runs in UTC. An order placed
    88	     at 00:30 in Berlin is billed on the day before.
    89	   - **Fix:** Compute the day once in the shop's time zone:
    90	     `datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Europe/Berlin")).date()`. One line, nothing else changes.
    91	   - **If we skip it:** Late orders show the wrong date, and accounting fixes them by hand.
    92	
    93	End with:
    94	- `Verdict: Ship.` or `Verdict: fix 1 and 3 first.`
    95	- `Lean: -<N> lines possible.` when lean findings exist.
    96	- `Not checked:` one line, if something mattered and you could not check it.
    97	
    98	Nothing found: `What this change does:`, then `Looks good. Ship.` and one line
    99	on what you checked.
   100	
   101	Lists findings, changes no code.

**1. Must fix: imports can escape the reviewed checkout and invalidate provenance.**

`benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py:30-33`:
```python
from chat import build_prompt, get_stop_tokens
import detect_person
from picker import URL
import relate
```

Importing `chat` executes `chat.py:3`:
```python
sys.path.insert(0, '/data/data/com.termux/files/home/robot')
```

Consequently, when that live directory is accessible, the subsequent imports can load `detect_person`, `picker`, and `relate` from the live repository instead of the isolated copy. The transitive `server_manager` import can escape too.

Meanwhile, `benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/quality1.py:614` hashes files relative to the harness checkout:
```python
        'code_sha256': {rel_p: relate.sha256(ROBOT / rel_p) for rel_p in imported_files},
```

This can record hashes for different code than actually executed. It also prevents the isolated tests from reliably checking the frozen reference implementation.

Fix this within the new candidate files: ensure the imported project modules resolve to the intended checkout despite `chat`’s path mutation, and verify/hash their actual source paths. Add a regression check for module origins without editing tracked files.

PIL renders the page text into pixels. A visibly printed variant label need not appear as an ASCII string in the PDF bytes, so these assertions can pass despite a leak. Capture drawing calls and inspect their text, or otherwise test the rendered content.
